The user wants to group iterations toward a single creative idea into a "workstream" so they can later see every iteration that fed a finished video.

- Default: new runs join the most recent workstream
- Warn / prompt when the user "switches type" so they don't accidentally pollute a stream

- Single `runs/.active` pointer for auto-nesting

- Is "workstream" even the right unit? Could it be a tree (workstream → branch → run) since iteration is often branching, not linear? Could it be a tag set instead of a folder?
- What's the failure mode of auto-nesting? Of manual nesting? Of tag-based?
- How do other tools solve this — git branches, Jupyter notebooks, Figma versions, ML experiment trackers (W&B, MLflow), generative art tools (ComfyUI history)? Pick the closest analogy and say why.

- What's the *minimum* metadata that lets you reconstruct "the iterations that fed this video"?

Give me a recommendation with the strongest single tradeoff called out. Be opinionated. Disagree with my pitch if you should.

# On the right primitive for grouping creative iterations

## The closest analogy is ML experiment trackers, not git

Git branches are the wrong model because branches are *intentional divergences with merge intent*. Creative iteration almost never merges — you pick a winner and discard the rest. Jupyter checkpoints are too implicit. Figma versions are linear and human-named, which violates "effortless."

The closest fit is **MLflow/W&B's "run inside experiment" model**: a flat namespace of runs, each carrying a foreign key to a parent "experiment," with the experiment being a thin label rather than a heavyweight container. Crucially, MLflow stores runs in their own location and the experiment is metadata, not a directory. This is the key insight you're missing.

## Disagreeing with the folder-nesting pitch

Nesting `runs/<workstream>/NNN-runslug/` couples *physical layout* to *logical grouping*. That's a load-bearing decision and it's wrong, because:

1. **Re-grouping becomes a `mv` operation** instead of a metadata edit. When the user realizes run 47 actually belonged to a different idea, you're moving directories and breaking any path-based references in timelines, logs, caches.
2. **It forces single-parent membership.** A run that's a "shared asset" (a base render reused across two ideas) has no clean home.
3. **`.active` pointer + folder coupling means switching workstreams is a stateful side-effect** — easy to forget, easy to corrupt.

**Tags are the right primitive.** Each run writes a `workstream_id` (ULID) into its own `run.json`. The workstream is reified lazily: a `workstreams/<id>.json` file appears the first time something needs to talk about the group. Folders stay flat. Re-grouping is a one-line edit.

## On auto-vs-declared

Auto-join-most-recent is correct as the *default*, but the failure mode is silent drift: user takes a 3-day break, comes back, makes a new thing, and it gets stapled to last week's idea. Your idle-timeout gate handles this. Good.

The type-switch warning is wrong-shaped, though — you correctly noted ideas cross tool types. Replace it with an **input-overlap heuristic**: if the new run shares no input artifacts (no upstream run from the active workstream, no shared source files), prompt. This catches "I started something new" without false-positives on the natural generate→cut→render flow.

## Minimum provenance

In the timeline, you need exactly: `workstream_id`, `workstream_label` (denormalized so it survives), and `contributing_run_ids: []`. Everything else is reconstructible.

## The single strongest tradeoff

**Tags-with-flat-layout costs you filesystem-level legibility** — you can no longer `ls runs/my-idea/` to see iterations; you need a `runs list --workstream my-idea` command. That's a real loss for a tool whose users live in the shell. But it buys you cheap re-grouping, multi-parent membership, and decoupling of "where bytes live" from "what they mean" — and those are the properties that determine whether the system survives contact with real creative workflows, where the user's mental model of "which idea is this" shifts *after* the bytes are written.

Take the legibility hit. Add a `runs tree` command to claw some of it back.

122	    if args.orchestrator_id:

129	def _cmd_run(args: argparse.Namespace, registry: OrchestratorRegistry) -> int:
130	    from .runner import OrchestratorRunRequest, run_orchestrator
131	
132	    request = OrchestratorRunRequest(
133	        orchestrator_id=args.orchestrator_id,
134	        out=Path(args.out) if args.out else None,
135	        inputs=_parse_input_values(args.input),
136	        brief=Path(args.brief) if args.brief else None,
137	        orchestrator_args=tuple(args.orchestrator_args),
138	        dry_run=bool(args.dry_run),
139	        python_exec=args.python_exec,
140	        verbose=bool(args.verbose),
141	    )
142	    result = run_orchestrator(request, registry)
143	    _print_run_result(result)
144	    return int(result.returncode or 0)
145	
146	
147	def _parse_input_values(raw_values: list[str]) -> dict[str, str]:
148	    values: dict[str, str] = {}
149	    for raw in raw_values:
150	        if "=" not in raw:
151	            raise ValueError(f"invalid --input value {raw!r}; expected NAME=VALUE")
152	        key, value = raw.split("=", 1)
153	        key = key.strip()
154	        if not key:
155	            raise ValueError(f"invalid --input value {raw!r}; expected NAME=VALUE")
156	        values[key] = value
157	    return values
158	
159	
160	def _split_run_passthrough(argv: list[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
161	    if not argv or argv[0] != "run" or "--" not in argv:
162	        return argv, []
163	    separator_index = argv.index("--")
164	    return argv[:separator_index], argv[separator_index + 1 :]
165	
166	
167	def _print_run_result(result: Any) -> None:
168	    commands = result.planned_commands or ((result.command,) if result.command else ())
169	    for command in commands:
170	        if command:
171	            print(shlex.join(command))
172	    if result.errors:
173	        for error in result.errors:
174	            print(f"{error.kind}: {error.message}", file=sys.stderr)
175	
176	
177	def _print_ports(label: str, ports: tuple[Any, ...]) -> None:
178	    if not ports:
179	        return
180	    print(f"{label}:")
181	    for port in ports:
182	        required = "required" if port.required else "optional"
183	        print(f"  - {port.name} ({port.type}, {required})")
184	
185	
186	def _print_outputs(orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition) -> None:
187	    if not orchestrator.outputs:
188	        return
189	    print("outputs:")
190	    for output in orchestrator.outputs:
191	        placeholder = f", placeholder={output.placeholder}" if output.placeholder else ""
192	        print(f"  - {output.name} ({output.type}, {output.mode}{placeholder})")
193	
194	
195	if __name__ == "__main__":
196	    raise SystemExit(main())

100	            missing_binaries=missing_binaries,
101	        )

104	        return _run_builtin_executor(executor, request)

138	def evaluate_conditions(executor: ExecutorDefinition, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> ConditionResult:
139	    for condition in executor.conditions:

149	
150	def build_pipeline_context(request: ExecutorRunRequest, executor: ExecutorDefinition | None = None) -> argparse.Namespace:
151	    pipeline = _pipeline_module()
152	    values = _request_values(request)
153	    out = Path(request.out).expanduser().resolve()
154	    brief = _optional_path(values.get("brief") or request.brief)
155	    if brief is None:
156	        brief = (out / "brief.txt").resolve()
157	    audio_value = values.get("audio")
158	    video_value = values.get("video")
159	    video = _optional_asset_path(video_value)
160	    audio = _optional_asset_path(audio_value if audio_value is not None else video_value)
161	    env_file = _optional_path(values.get("env_file"))
162	    theme_raw = values.get("theme")
163	    theme_explicit = theme_raw is not None
164	    theme = pipeline._resolve_theme_arg(theme_raw) if theme_explicit else pipeline._resolve_theme_arg(pipeline.WORKSPACE_ROOT / "themes" / "banodoco-default" / "theme.json")
165	    brief_slug = str(values.get("brief_slug") or _default_brief_slug(brief, out))
166	    brief_out = (out / "briefs" / brief_slug).resolve()
167	    skip = _as_string_list(values.get("skip"))
168	    asset_values = _as_string_list(values.get("asset") or values.get("assets"))
169	    args = argparse.Namespace(
170	        audio=audio,
171	        video=video,
172	        out=out,
173	        brief=brief,
174	        brief_out=brief_out,
175	        brief_copy=brief_out / "brief.txt",
176	        skip=skip,
177	        asset=asset_values,
178	        asset_pairs=_parse_asset_pairs(asset_values),
179	        primary_asset=values.get("primary_asset"),
180	        theme=theme,
181	        theme_explicit=theme_explicit,
182	        source_slug=str(values.get("source_slug") or out.name),
183	        brief_slug=brief_slug,
184	        env_file=env_file,
185	        extra_args=_normalize_extra_args(values.get("extra_args")),
186	        target_duration=_optional_float(values.get("target_duration")),
187	        python_exec=str(values.get("python_exec") or request.python_exec or sys.executable),
188	        render=bool(values.get("render", False)),
189	        verbose=bool(values.get("verbose", request.verbose)),
190	        no_prefetch=bool(values.get("no_prefetch", False)),
191	        keep_downloads=bool(values.get("keep_downloads", False)),
192	        cache_dir=_optional_path(values.get("cache_dir")),
193	        drift=str(values.get("drift") or "strict"),
194	        from_step=values.get("from_step"),
195	        max_editor_passes=int(values.get("max_editor_passes", 2)),
196	        editor_iteration=int(values.get("editor_iteration", 1)),
197	    )
198	    if executor is not None:
199	        args.executor_id = executor.id
200	    return args
201	
202	
203	def build_executor_command(request: ExecutorRunRequest, registry: ExecutorRegistry | None = None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
204	    active_registry = registry or load_default_registry()
205	    executor = active_registry.get(request.executor_id)
206	    values = _request_values(request)
207	    _validate_required_inputs(executor, values)
208	    condition_result = evaluate_conditions(executor, values)
209	    if condition_result.skipped:
210	        return ()
211	    if executor.kind == "built_in" and "pipeline_step" in executor.metadata:
212	        step = _step_for_executor(executor)
213	        args = build_pipeline_context(request, executor)
214	        return tuple(step.build_cmd(args))
215	    return _expand_external_command(executor, request, values)[0]
216	
217	
218	def _run_builtin_executor(executor: ExecutorDefinition, request: ExecutorRunRequest) -> ExecutorRunResult:
219	    pipeline = _pipeline_module()
220	    step = _step_for_executor(executor)
221	    args = build_pipeline_context(request, executor)
222	    command = tuple(step.build_cmd(args))
223	    if request.dry_run:
224	        return ExecutorRunResult(
225	            executor_id=executor.id,
226	            kind=executor.kind,
227	            command=command,
228	            payload={"executor_id": executor.id, "missing_binaries": [], "returncode": None, "skipped": False, "skipped_reason": ""},
229	            dry_run=True,
230	        )
231	    if args.brief.exists():
232	        pipeline.prepare_brief_artifacts(args)
233	    returncode = pipeline.run_step(step, list(command), args)
234	    return ExecutorRunResult(
235	        executor_id=executor.id,
236	        kind=executor.kind,
237	        command=command,
238	        payload={"executor_id": executor.id, "missing_binaries": [], "returncode": returncode, "skipped": False, "skipped_reason": ""},
239	        returncode=returncode,
240	    )
241	
242	
243	def _run_external_executor(executor: ExecutorDefinition, request: ExecutorRunRequest, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> ExecutorRunResult:
244	    command, cwd, env = _expand_external_command(executor, request, values)
245	    if request.dry_run:
246	        return ExecutorRunResult(
247	            executor_id=executor.id,
248	            kind=executor.kind,
249	            command=command,
250	            cwd=cwd,
251	            env=env,
252	            payload={"executor_id": executor.id, "missing_binaries": [], "returncode": None, "skipped": False, "skipped_reason": ""},
253	            dry_run=True,
254	        )
255	    completed = subprocess.run(
256	        list(command),
257	        cwd=cwd,
258	        env={**os.environ, **env},
259	        check=False,
260	    )
261	    return ExecutorRunResult(
262	        executor_id=executor.id,
263	        kind=executor.kind,
264	        command=command,
265	        cwd=cwd,
266	        env=env,
267	        payload={
268	            "executor_id": executor.id,
269	            "missing_binaries": [],
270	            "returncode": completed.returncode,
271	            "skipped": False,
272	            "skipped_reason": "",
273	        },
274	        returncode=completed.returncode,
275	    )
276	
277	
278	def _expand_external_command(
279	    executor: ExecutorDefinition,
280	    request: ExecutorRunRequest,
281	    values: Mapping[str, Any],
282	) -> tuple[tuple[str, ...], str | None, dict[str, str]]:
283	    if executor.command is None:
284	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"executor {executor.id!r} has no command")
285	    placeholders = _placeholder_values(executor, request, values)
286	    argv = tuple(_expand_placeholders(part, placeholders) for part in executor.command.argv)
287	    cwd = _expand_placeholders(executor.command.cwd, placeholders) if executor.command.cwd else None
288	    env = {[REDACTED](value, placeholders) for key, value in executor.command.env.items()}
289	    return argv, cwd, env
290	
291	
292	def _placeholder_values(executor: ExecutorDefinition, request: ExecutorRunRequest, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, str]:
293	    out = Path(request.out).expanduser().resolve()
294	    placeholders: dict[str, str] = {
295	        "out": str(out),
296	    }
297	    python_exec = _resolve_python_exec(executor, request, values)
298	    if python_exec is not None:
299	        placeholders["python_exec"] = python_exec
300	    brief = values.get("brief") or request.brief
301	    if brief is not None:
302	        brief_path = Path(str(brief)).expanduser().resolve()
303	        placeholders["brief"] = str(brief_path)
304	        brief_slug = str(values.get("brief_slug") or _default_brief_slug(brief_path, out))
305	        brief_out = out / "briefs" / brief_slug
306	        placeholders["brief_slug"] = brief_slug
307	        placeholders["brief_out"] = str(brief_out)
308	        placeholders["brief_copy"] = str(brief_out / "brief.txt")
309	    for key, value in values.items():
310	        if value is None:
311	            continue
312	        placeholders[key] = _stringify_value(value)
313	    for output in executor.outputs:
314	        output_path = _output_value(output, request, placeholders)
315	        placeholders[output.name] = output_path
316	        if output.placeholder:
317	            placeholders[output.placeholder] = output_path
318	    return placeholders
319	
320	
321	def _output_value(output: ExecutorOutput, request: ExecutorRunRequest, placeholders: Mapping[str, str]) -> str:
322	    if output.name in request.outputs:
323	        return _stringify_value(request.outputs[output.name])
324	    if output.placeholder and output.placeholder in request.outputs:
325	        return _stringify_value(request.outputs[output.placeholder])
326	    if output.path_template:
327	        return _expand_placeholders(output.path_template, placeholders)
328	    return str((Path(request.out).expanduser().resolve() / output.name).resolve())
329	
330	
331	def _resolve_python_exec(executor: ExecutorDefinition, request: ExecutorRunRequest, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str | None:
332	    input_override = values.get("python_exec")
333	    if _has_value(input_override):
334	        return str(input_override)
335	    if _has_value(request.python_exec):
336	        return str(request.python_exec)
337	    if not _executor_uses_placeholder(executor, "python_exec"):
338	        return None
339	    if executor.kind == "external" and executor.isolation.mode == "subprocess":
340	        installed_python = executor_python_path(executor)
341	        if installed_python.is_file():
342	            return str(installed_python)
343	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(
344	            f"executor {executor.id!r} requires an installed Python environment; "
345	            f"run `python3 -m artagents executors install {executor.id}` or pass python_exec as an input override"
346	        )
347	    return sys.executable
348	
349	
350	def _executor_uses_placeholder(executor: ExecutorDefinition, placeholder: str) -> bool:
351	    if executor.command is None:
352	        return False
353	    needle = f"{{{placeholder}}}"
354	    if any(needle in part for part in executor.command.argv):
355	        return True
356	    if executor.command.cwd and needle in executor.command.cwd:
357	        return True
358	    return any(needle in value for value in executor.command.env.values())
359	
360	
361	def _expand_placeholders(value: str, placeholders: Mapping[str, str]) -> str:
362	    def replace(match: re.Match[str]) -> str:
363	        key = match.group(1)
364	        if key not in placeholders:
365	            raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"missing value for placeholder {{{key}}}")
366	        return placeholders[key]
367	
368	    return _PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(replace, value)
369	
370	
371	def _validate_required_inputs(executor: ExecutorDefinition, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
372	    missing = [
373	        port.name
374	        for port in executor.inputs
375	        if port.required and port.default is None and not _has_value(values.get(port.name))
376	    ]
377	    if missing:
378	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"executor {executor.id!r} missing required input(s): {', '.join(missing)}")
379	
380	
381	def _evaluate_condition(condition: ConditionSpec, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> ConditionResult:
382	    if condition.kind == "always":
383	        return ConditionResult()
384	    if condition.kind == "requires_input":
385	        if not condition.input or not _has_value(values.get(condition.input)):
386	            raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"condition requires input {condition.input!r}")
387	        return ConditionResult()
388	    if condition.kind == "requires_file":
389	        candidate = values.get(condition.input) if condition.input else condition.path
390	        if not _has_value(candidate):
391	            raise ExecutorRunnerError("condition requires a file path")
392	        path = Path(str(candidate)).expanduser()
393	        if not path.is_file():
394	            raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"condition requires file: {path}")
395	        return ConditionResult()
396	    if condition.kind == "skip_if_input" and condition.input and _has_value(values.get(condition.input)):
397	        return ConditionResult(skipped=True, reason=f"input {condition.input!r} is set")
398	    raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"unsupported condition kind {condition.kind!r}")
399	
400	
401	def _step_for_executor(executor: ExecutorDefinition) -> Any:
402	    step_name = executor.metadata.get("pipeline_step")
403	    if not isinstance(step_name, str):
404	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"built-in executor {executor.id!r} is missing metadata.pipeline_step")
405	    steps = _builtin_steps_by_name()
406	    if step_name not in steps:
407	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"built-in executor {executor.id!r} references unknown pipeline step {step_name!r}")
408	    return steps[step_name]
409	
410	
411	def _request_values(request: ExecutorRunRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
412	    values = dict(request.inputs)
413	    if request.brief is not None and "brief" not in values:
414	        values["brief"] = request.brief
415	    if request.python_exec is not None and "python_exec" not in values:
416	        values["python_exec"] = request.python_exec
417	    values.setdefault("verbose", request.verbose)
418	    return values
419	
420	
421	def _has_value(value: Any) -> bool:
422	    return value is not None and value != ""
423	
424	
425	def _optional_path(value: Any) -> Path | None:
426	    if value is None or value == "":
427	        return None
428	    return Path(str(value)).expanduser().resolve()
429	
430	
431	def _optional_asset_path(value: Any) -> Path | str | None:
432	    if value is None or value == "":
433	        return None
434	    text = str(value)
435	    pipeline = _pipeline_module()
436	    if pipeline.asset_cache.is_url(text):
437	        return text
438	    return Path(text).expanduser().resolve()
439	
440	
441	def _optional_float(value: Any) -> float | None:
442	    if value is None or value == "":
443	        return None
444	    return float(value)
445	
446	
447	def _as_string_list(value: Any) -> list[str]:
448	    if value is None or value == "":
449	        return []
450	    if isinstance(value, str):
451	        return [value]
452	    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
453	        return [str(item) for item in value]
454	    return [str(value)]
455	
456	
457	def _parse_asset_pairs(values: list[str]) -> list[tuple[str, Path | str]]:
458	    pairs: list[tuple[str, Path | str]] = []
459	    for raw in values:
460	        if "=" not in raw:
461	            raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"invalid asset value {raw!r}; expected KEY=PATH")
462	        key, path_text = raw.split("=", 1)
463	        key = key.strip()
464	        path_text = path_text.strip()
465	        if not key or not path_text:
466	            raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"invalid asset value {raw!r}; expected KEY=PATH")
467	        pipeline = _pipeline_module()
468	        if pipeline.asset_cache.is_url(path_text):
469	            pairs.append((key, path_text))
470	        else:
471	            pairs.append((key, Path(path_text).expanduser().resolve()))
472	    return pairs
473	
474	
475	def _normalize_extra_args(value: Any) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
476	    if value is None:
477	        return {}
478	    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
479	        raise ExecutorRunnerError("extra_args must be an object keyed by step name")
480	    return {str(key): _as_string_list(raw_values) for key, raw_values in value.items()}
481	
482	
483	def _default_brief_slug(brief: Path, out: Path) -> str:
484	    generic_brief_names = {"brief", "plan", "prompt"}
485	    return out.name if brief.stem.lower() in generic_brief_names else brief.stem
486	
487	
488	def _stringify_value(value: Any) -> str:
489	    if isinstance(value, Path):
490	        return str(value)
491	    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
492	        return ",".join(str(item) for item in value)
493	    return str(value)
494	
495	
496	def _required_input(inputs: Mapping[str, Any], key: str) -> str:
497	    value = inputs.get(key)
498	    if value in (None, ""):
499	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"{key} is required")
500	    return str(value)
501	
502	
503	def _optional_input(inputs: Mapping[str, Any], key: str) -> Any:
504	    value = inputs.get(key)
505	    if value in (None, ""):
506	        return None
507	    return value
508	
509	
510	__all__ = [
511	    "ConditionResult",
512	    "ExecutorRunRequest",
513	    "ExecutorRunResult",
514	    "ExecutorRunnerError",
515	    "build_pipeline_context",
516	    "build_executor_command",
517	    "check_executor_binaries",
518	    "evaluate_conditions",
519	    "run_executor",
520	]
521

120	def run_orchestrator(request: OrchestratorRunRequest, registry: OrchestratorRegistry | None = None) -> OrchestratorRunResult:
121	    active_registry = registry or load_default_registry()
122	    orchestrator = active_registry.get(request.orchestrator_id)
123	    values = _request_values(request)
124	    _validate_out_requirement(orchestrator, request)
125	    _validate_required_inputs(orchestrator, values)
126	    if orchestrator.runtime.kind == "python":
127	        return _ensure_dry_run_plan(_run_python_orchestrator(orchestrator, request))
128	    if orchestrator.runtime.kind == "command":
129	        return _ensure_dry_run_plan(_run_command_orchestrator(orchestrator, request, values))
130	    raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"unsupported orchestrator runtime kind {orchestrator.runtime.kind!r}")
131	
132	
133	def build_orchestrator_command(request: OrchestratorRunRequest, registry: OrchestratorRegistry | None = None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
134	    active_registry = registry or load_default_registry()
135	    orchestrator = active_registry.get(request.orchestrator_id)
136	    values = _request_values(request)
137	    _validate_out_requirement(orchestrator, request)
138	    _validate_required_inputs(orchestrator, values)
139	    if orchestrator.runtime.kind != "command":
140	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"orchestrator {orchestrator.id!r} does not use a command runtime")
141	    command, _, _ = _expand_command_runtime(orchestrator, request, values)
142	    return command
143	
144	
145	def _run_python_orchestrator(orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition, request: OrchestratorRunRequest) -> OrchestratorRunResult:
146	    runtime = orchestrator.runtime
147	    if not runtime.module or not runtime.function:
148	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"orchestrator {orchestrator.id!r} has an invalid Python runtime")
149	    try:
150	        module = importlib.import_module(runtime.module)
151	    except Exception as exc:
152	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"failed to import orchestrator runtime module {runtime.module!r}: {exc}") from exc
153	    target = getattr(module, runtime.function, None)
154	    if not callable(target):
155	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"orchestrator runtime target {runtime.module}.{runtime.function} is not callable")
156	    try:
157	        raw_result = target(request, orchestrator)
158	    except OrchestratorRunnerError:
159	        raise
160	    except Exception as exc:
161	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"orchestrator {orchestrator.id!r} Python runtime failed: {exc}") from exc
162	    return _normalize_python_result(orchestrator, request, raw_result)
163	
164	
165	def _run_command_orchestrator(
166	    orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition,
167	    request: OrchestratorRunRequest,
168	    values: Mapping[str, Any],
169	) -> OrchestratorRunResult:
170	    command, cwd, env = _expand_command_runtime(orchestrator, request, values)
171	    if request.dry_run:
172	        planned_commands = (command,)
173	        return OrchestratorRunResult(
174	            orchestrator_id=orchestrator.id,
175	            kind=orchestrator.kind,
176	            runtime_kind="command",
177	            command=command,
178	            planned_commands=planned_commands,
179	            cwd=cwd,
180	            env=env,
181	            returncode=None,
182	            dry_run=True,
183	            plan=_plan_from_commands(planned_commands, prefix=orchestrator.id),
184	        )
185	    completed = subprocess.run(
186	        list(command),
187	        cwd=cwd,
188	        env={**os.environ, **env},
189	        check=False,
190	    )
191	    return OrchestratorRunResult(
192	        orchestrator_id=orchestrator.id,
193	        kind=orchestrator.kind,
194	        runtime_kind="command",
195	        command=command,
196	        planned_commands=(command,),
197	        cwd=cwd,
198	        env=env,
199	        returncode=completed.returncode,
200	    )
201	
202	
203	def _expand_command_runtime(
204	    orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition,
205	    request: OrchestratorRunRequest,
206	    values: Mapping[str, Any],
207	) -> tuple[tuple[str, ...], str | None, dict[str, str]]:
208	    command_spec = orchestrator.runtime.command
209	    if command_spec is None:
210	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"orchestrator {orchestrator.id!r} has no command runtime")
211	    placeholders = _placeholder_values(orchestrator, request, values)
212	    argv: list[str] = []
213	    for part in command_spec.argv:
214	        if part == "{orchestrator_args}":
215	            argv.extend(request.orchestrator_args)
216	        else:
217	            argv.append(_expand_placeholders(part, placeholders))
218	    cwd = _expand_placeholders(command_spec.cwd, placeholders) if command_spec.cwd else None
219	    env = {[REDACTED](value, placeholders) for key, value in command_spec.env.items()}
220	    return tuple(argv), cwd, env
221	
222	
223	def _normalize_python_result(
224	    orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition,
225	    request: OrchestratorRunRequest,
226	    raw_result: Any,
227	) -> OrchestratorRunResult:
228	    if isinstance(raw_result, OrchestratorRunResult):
229	        return _ensure_dry_run_plan(raw_result)
230	    if raw_result is None:
231	        return _ensure_dry_run_plan(OrchestratorRunResult(
232	            orchestrator_id=orchestrator.id,
233	            kind=orchestrator.kind,
234	            runtime_kind="python",
235	            returncode=None if request.dry_run else 0,
236	            dry_run=request.dry_run,
237	        ))
238	    if isinstance(raw_result, int):
239	        return _ensure_dry_run_plan(OrchestratorRunResult(
240	            orchestrator_id=orchestrator.id,
241	            kind=orchestrator.kind,
242	            runtime_kind="python",
243	            returncode=None if request.dry_run else raw_result,
244	            dry_run=request.dry_run,
245	        ))
246	    if isinstance(raw_result, dict):
247	        return _result_from_mapping(orchestrator, request, raw_result)
248	    raise OrchestratorRunnerError(
249	        f"orchestrator {orchestrator.id!r} returned unsupported result type {type(raw_result).__name__}; "
250	        "expected OrchestratorRunResult, dict, int, or None"
251	    )
252	
253	
254	def _result_from_mapping(
255	    orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition,
256	    request: OrchestratorRunRequest,
257	    raw: Mapping[str, Any],
258	) -> OrchestratorRunResult:
259	    command = _tuple_of_strings(raw.get("command", ()), "command")
260	    planned_commands = _planned_commands(raw.get("planned_commands", (command,) if command else ()))
261	    errors = tuple(
262	        error if isinstance(error, OrchestratorRunError) else OrchestratorRunError(str(error))
263	        for error in raw.get("errors", ())
264	    )
265	    returncode = raw.get("returncode")
266	    if request.dry_run and returncode is not None:
267	        returncode = None
268	    elif returncode is not None:
269	        returncode = int(returncode)
270	    plan = _plan_from_raw(raw.get("plan")) if "plan" in raw else None
271	    return _ensure_dry_run_plan(OrchestratorRunResult(
272	        orchestrator_id=str(raw.get("orchestrator_id") or orchestrator.id),
273	        kind=str(raw.get("kind") or orchestrator.kind),
274	        runtime_kind=str(raw.get("runtime_kind") or "python"),
275	        command=command,
276	        planned_commands=planned_commands,
277	        cwd=_optional_string(raw.get("cwd")),
278	        env={str(key): str(value) for key, value in dict(raw.get("env", {})).items()},
279	        returncode=returncode,
280	        dry_run=bool(raw.get("dry_run", request.dry_run)),
281	        outputs=dict(raw.get("outputs", {})),
282	        errors=errors,
283	        plan=plan,
284	    ))
285	
286	
287	def _ensure_dry_run_plan(result: OrchestratorRunResult) -> OrchestratorRunResult:
288	    if not result.dry_run or result.plan is not None:
289	        return result
290	    return replace(result, plan=_plan_from_commands(result.planned_commands, prefix=result.orchestrator_id))
291	
292	
293	def _plan_from_commands(commands: tuple[tuple[str, ...], ...], *, prefix: str) -> OrchestratorPlan:
294	    steps = tuple(
295	        OrchestratorPlanStep(
296	            id=f"{prefix}.step_{index + 1}",
297	            kind="command",
298	            command=command,
299	        )
300	        for index, command in enumerate(commands)
301	    )
302	    return OrchestratorPlan(steps=steps)
303	
304	
305	def _plan_from_raw(raw: Any) -> OrchestratorPlan | None:
306	    if raw is None:
307	        return None
308	    if isinstance(raw, OrchestratorPlan):
309	        return raw
310	    if not isinstance(raw, Mapping):
311	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError("plan must be an object")
312	    raw_steps = raw.get("steps", ())
313	    if not isinstance(raw_steps, (list, tuple)):
314	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError("plan.steps must be a list")
315	    return OrchestratorPlan(
316	        steps=tuple(_plan_step_from_raw(item, f"plan.steps[{index}]") for index, item in enumerate(raw_steps)),
317	        summary=str(raw.get("summary") or ""),
318	    )
319	
320	
321	def _plan_step_from_raw(raw: Any, path: str) -> OrchestratorPlanStep:
322	    if isinstance(raw, OrchestratorPlanStep):
323	        return raw
324	    if not isinstance(raw, Mapping):
325	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"{path} must be an object")
326	    step_id = raw.get("id")
327	    if not isinstance(step_id, str) or not step_id.strip():
328	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"{path}.id must be a non-empty string")
329	    return OrchestratorPlanStep(
330	        id=step_id,
331	        kind=str(raw.get("kind") or "command"),
332	        command=_tuple_of_strings(raw.get("command", ()), f"{path}.command"),
333	        description=str(raw.get("description") or ""),
334	        metadata=dict(raw.get("metadata", {})),
335	    )
336	
337	
338	def _placeholder_values(orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition, request: OrchestratorRunRequest, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, str]:
339	    placeholders: dict[str, str] = {
340	        "python_exec": str(values.get("python_exec") or request.python_exec or sys.executable),
341	        "orchestrator_args": " ".join(request.orchestrator_args),
342	        "verbose": str(bool(values.get("verbose", request.verbose))).lower(),
343	    }
344	    if request.out is not None:
345	        placeholders["out"] = str(Path(request.out).expanduser().resolve())
346	    brief = values.get("brief") or request.brief
347	    if brief is not None:
348	        placeholders["brief"] = str(Path(str(brief)).expanduser().resolve())
349	    for key, value in values.items():
350	        if value is None:
351	            continue
352	        if key == "verbose":
353	            placeholders[key] = str(bool(value)).lower()
354	        else:
355	            placeholders[key] = _stringify_value(value)
356	    for output in orchestrator.outputs:
357	        output_path = _output_value(output, request, placeholders)
358	        placeholders[output.name] = output_path
359	        if output.placeholder:
360	            placeholders[output.placeholder] = output_path
361	    return placeholders
362	
363	
364	def _output_value(output: Output, request: OrchestratorRunRequest, placeholders: Mapping[str, str]) -> str:
365	    if output.name in request.outputs:
366	        return _stringify_value(request.outputs[output.name])
367	    if output.placeholder and output.placeholder in request.outputs:
368	        return _stringify_value(request.outputs[output.placeholder])
369	    if output.path_template:
370	        return _expand_placeholders(output.path_template, placeholders)
371	    if request.out is None:
372	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"--out is required to derive output {output.name!r}")
373	    return str((Path(request.out).expanduser().resolve() / output.name).resolve())
374	
375	
376	def _expand_placeholders(value: str, placeholders: Mapping[str, str]) -> str:
377	    def replace(match: re.Match[str]) -> str:
378	        key = match.group(1)
379	        if key not in placeholders:
380	            raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"missing value for placeholder {{{key}}}")
381	        return placeholders[key]
382	
383	    return _PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(replace, value)
384	
385	
386	def _validate_required_inputs(orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
387	    missing = [
388	        port.name
389	        for port in orchestrator.inputs
390	        if port.required and port.default is None and not _has_value(values.get(port.name))
391	    ]
392	    if missing:
393	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"orchestrator {orchestrator.id!r} missing required input(s): {', '.join(missing)}")
394	
395	
396	def _validate_out_requirement(orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition, request: OrchestratorRunRequest) -> None:
397	    if request.out is not None:
398	        return
399	    if orchestrator.id == "builtin.thumbnail_maker":
400	        return
401	    if not request.dry_run:
402	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError("--out is required for orchestrator execution")
403	    if orchestrator.id == "builtin.event_talks":
404	        return
405	    if orchestrator.id == "builtin.hype":
406	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError("--out is required for builtin.hype dry-run")
407	    if orchestrator.runtime.kind == "command" and _command_runtime_requires_out(orchestrator, request):
408	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError("--out is required for command runtime placeholders")
409	
410	
411	def _command_runtime_requires_out(orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition, request: OrchestratorRunRequest) -> bool:
412	    command = orchestrator.runtime.command
413	    if command is None:
414	        return False
415	    values = [*command.argv]
416	    if command.cwd:
417	        values.append(command.cwd)
418	    values.extend(command.env.values())
419	    if any(_uses_placeholder(value, "out") for value in values):
420	        return True
421	    for output in orchestrator.outputs:
422	        if output.name in request.outputs or (output.placeholder and output.placeholder in request.outputs):
423	            continue
424	        if output.path_template is None or _uses_placeholder(output.path_template, "out"):
425	            return True
426	    return False
427	
428	
429	def _uses_placeholder(value: str, placeholder: str) -> bool:
430	    return placeholder in _PLACEHOLDER_RE.findall(value)
431	
432	
433	def _request_values(request: OrchestratorRunRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
434	    values = dict(request.inputs)
435	    if request.brief is not None and "brief" not in values:
436	        values["brief"] = request.brief
437	    if request.python_exec is not None and "python_exec" not in values:
438	        values["python_exec"] = request.python_exec
439	    values.setdefault("verbose", request.verbose)
440	    return values
441	
442	
443	def _planned_commands(raw: Any) -> tuple[tuple[str, ...], ...]:
444	    if raw is None:
445	        return ()
446	    if not isinstance(raw, (list, tuple)):
447	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError("planned_commands must be a list of command lists")
448	    commands: list[tuple[str, ...]] = []
449	    for index, item in enumerate(raw):
450	        commands.append(_tuple_of_strings(item, f"planned_commands[{index}]"))
451	    return tuple(commands)
452	
453	
454	def _tuple_of_strings(raw: Any, path: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
455	    if raw is None or raw == ():
456	        return ()
457	    if not isinstance(raw, (list, tuple)):
458	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"{path} must be a list")
459	    result: list[str] = []
460	    for index, item in enumerate(raw):
461	        if not isinstance(item, str):
462	            raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"{path}[{index}] must be a string")
463	        result.append(item)
464	    return tuple(result)
465	
466	
467	def _optional_string(value: Any) -> str | None:
468	    if value is None:
469	        return None
470	    return str(value)
471	
472	
473	__all__ = [
474	    "OrchestratorRunError",
475	    "OrchestratorRunRequest",
476	    "OrchestratorRunResult",
477	    "OrchestratorRunnerError",
478	    "build_orchestrator_command",
479	    "run_orchestrator",
480	]
481

# Workstreams: match the maker's actual head-state

## The mental model is wrong

Creators don't think in "workstreams." They don't think in "sessions" either — sessions are a developer abstraction borrowed from web analytics. What a maker actually thinks in is **"the thing I'm chasing right now."** It's a *pursuit*, not a container. It has a vibe, not a boundary. They abandon it for lunch, come back, drift sideways into a tangent that becomes the real idea, and only retroactively realize the tangent was the point.

Your `.active` pointer + status gate is technically correct and emotionally wrong. It asks the user to maintain state ("is my pursuit still open?") that they don't maintain in their own head. Idle timeout is the right instinct but the wrong trigger — people walk away mid-thought constantly.

## Drop prompts entirely. Group everything, split later.

**Auto-group every run into the previous one.** No prompts. No warnings on type switch — type switches are the *interesting* signal that a pursuit is evolving, not breaking. Don't punish them.

The killer move is a **retroactive timeline view**: a horizontal strip of every run from the last N days, with subtle visual breaks where the system *thinks* a new pursuit started (long gap, prompt-embedding drift, output-type pivot, explicit save). The user drags handles to merge or split. This is the no-prompt design — the decision moves from *during* flow to *after* flow, where it belongs.

## Naming: never up-front. Lazy and stolen.

Auto-name from the first prompt's first 4 salient words. Always editable, never required. The moment the user does anything that smells like commitment — saves a render, exports, commits — *that* artifact's filename becomes the pursuit's name. Names are a side effect of caring, not a precondition for working.

## Branching: don't build it. Yet.

Linear list. v3-from-v1 is just "run with parent_run_id." Store the parent edge, render the timeline as a tree if it branches, but don't make the user *think* about branches. 90% of pursuits are linear; the 10% that fork will fork visually on their own once the data is there.

## Closing: never explicitly. Three implicit signals, ranked.

1. Save/export/commit a final artifact -> pursuit auto-archives but stays editable
2. 48h of silence -> pursuit greys out but auto-reopens on next related run
3. User explicitly starts a "new pursuit" -> rare, honored

No "close" button in the primary flow.

## The 6-month killer demo

Open the finished video. One click: **"show me the 47 runs that led here, in order, with the prompt diffs and which ones I reused."** That's the whole product. Everything else is plumbing to make that view honest.

## Verdict on your pitch

`.active` pointer: keep, it's free. Status open/closed gate: cut it, replace with parent-edge + retroactive split UI. Type-switch warning: definitely cut — it pathologizes the most creative move a maker makes.

I need to understand the **CLI surface, user-facing UX, and persistence conventions** of this repo so I can design a "workstream" grouping layer that nests runs by a single creative idea. Read code, don't write.

**What I need you to find:**

1. **CLI surface today.** What top-level subcommands exist on `python3 -m artagents`? (e.g. `executors`, `orchestrators`, `elements`, `setup`, `doctor`, ...) Find the dispatcher file and list them. Where would a `workstream` subcommand live?

2. **State / persistence outside of `runs/`.** Are there hidden dirs like `.artagents/`, config files, registries, caches? What persists between invocations? Is there any precedent for "active session" / "current project" pointers?

3. **How does the user discover what they made?** Is there a "list past runs" command? A way to browse history? If not, what's the user's current workflow for finding "the v3 logo I made last week"? Look for any `list`, `history`, `show`, `inspect` commands beyond the tool catalog.

4. **Slug / naming conventions.** Look at how runs are named today (`artagents_logo`, `artagents_logo_v2`, `first-rite`, `creative_idea`). Is there a sluggify util? Auto-versioning? Collision handling? Find the code.

5. **`.gitignore` and what's tracked.** What lives in `runs/` is ignored — but is *anything* under `runs/` ever tracked? Is there a convention for "this run is worth keeping" vs ephemeral? Look at `.gitignore`.

6. **Existing tests around run lifecycle / persistence.** Find tests that exercise the runner end-to-end. They'll tell me what behaviors are guaranteed and where workstream nesting might break assumptions.

7. **Onboarding / docs.** Skim `README.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `docs/creating-tools.md`. Is there a documented user workflow ("first you do X, then Y") that a workstream concept would either fit naturally into or violate?

Report findings with file:line references. Under 500 words. I want concrete leverage points, not generalities — where can a workstream layer slot in without touching every tool.

82	def _resolve_theme_arg(value: object) -> Path:
83	    """Resolve --theme as either a theme.json path, a theme directory, or a workspace theme slug."""
84	    candidate = Path(str(value)).expanduser()
85	    if candidate.name == "theme.json":
86	        return candidate.resolve()
87	    if candidate.exists():
88	        if candidate.is_dir():
89	            return (candidate / "theme.json").resolve()
90	        return candidate.resolve()
91	    workspace_themes = WORKSPACE_ROOT / "themes"
92	    return (workspace_themes / str(value) / "theme.json").resolve()

201	def load_config(path_text: str) -> dict:
202	    path = Path(path_text).expanduser().resolve()
203	    if not path.is_file():
204	        usage_error(f"artagents: config file not found: {path}")
205	    suffix = path.suffix.lower()
206	    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
207	    if suffix == ".json":
208	        try:
209	            data = json.loads(text)
210	        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
211	            usage_error(f"artagents: invalid JSON config {path}: {exc.msg}")
212	    elif suffix in {".yaml", ".yml"}:
213	        if yaml is None:
214	            usage_error(f"artagents: YAML config requires PyYAML: {path}")
215	        try:
216	            data = yaml.safe_load(text)
217	        except Exception as exc:
218	            usage_error(f"artagents: invalid YAML config {path}: {exc}")
219	    else:
220	        try:
221	            data = json.loads(text)
222	        except json.JSONDecodeError:
223	            if yaml is None:
224	                usage_error(f"artagents: unsupported config format for {path}; use JSON or install PyYAML for YAML")
225	            try:
226	                data = yaml.safe_load(text)
227	            except Exception as exc:
228	                usage_error(f"artagents: invalid config {path}: {exc}")
229	    if data is None:
230	        return {}
231	    if not isinstance(data, dict):
232	        usage_error(f"artagents: config must decode to an object: {path}")
233	    return data
234	
235	
236	def normalize_config(raw: dict) -> dict:
237	    data = dict(raw)
238	    if "from" in data and "from_step" not in data:
239	        data["from_step"] = data.pop("from")
240	    if "python" in data and "python_exec" not in data:
241	        data["python_exec"] = data.pop("python")
242	    if "assets" in data and "asset" not in data:
243	        data["asset"] = data.pop("assets")
244	    return data
245	
246	
247	def parse_asset_entry(raw: str) -> tuple[str, Path | str]:
248	    if "=" not in raw:
249	        usage_error(f"artagents: invalid --asset value {raw!r}; expected KEY=PATH")
250	    key, path_text = raw.split("=", 1)
251	    key = key.strip()
252	    path_text = path_text.strip()
253	    if not key or not path_text:
254	        usage_error(f"artagents: invalid --asset value {raw!r}; expected KEY=PATH")
255	    if key == "main":
256	        usage_error("artagents: asset key 'main' is reserved; pass the primary video via --video")
257	    if asset_cache.is_url(path_text):
258	        return key, path_text
259	    path = Path(path_text).expanduser().resolve()
260	    if not path.exists():
261	        usage_error(f"artagents: asset path not found for {key!r}: {path}")
262	    return key, path
263	
264	
265	def normalize_many(raw: object, *, key_name: str) -> list[str]:
266	    if raw is None:
267	        return []
268	    if isinstance(raw, str):
269	        return [raw]
270	    if not isinstance(raw, list) or not all(isinstance(item, str) for item in raw):
271	        usage_error(f"artagents: {key_name} must be a string or list of strings")
272	    return list(raw)

274	
275	def normalize_extra_args(raw: object) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
276	    if raw is None:
277	        return {}
278	    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
279	        usage_error("artagents: extra_args must be an object keyed by step name")
280	    allowed_steps = set(STEP_ORDER)
281	    extra_args: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
282	    for step_name, values in raw.items():
283	        if step_name not in allowed_steps:
284	            usage_error(f"artagents: unknown extra_args step {step_name!r}")
285	        if not isinstance(values, list) or not all(isinstance(item, (str, int, float)) for item in values):
286	            usage_error(f"artagents: extra_args[{step_name!r}] must be a list of CLI tokens")
287	        extra_args[step_name] = [str(item) for item in values]
288	    return extra_args

1	"""Local setup planner for ArtAgents."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import argparse
6	import json
7	from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
8	from pathlib import Path
9	
10	from artagents._paths import REPO_ROOT
11	from artagents.elements.cli import _sync_managed_defaults
12	from artagents.elements.install import install_element
13	from artagents.elements.registry import load_default_registry as load_element_registry
14	
15	
16	@dataclass(frozen=True)
17	class SetupStep:
18	    name: str
19	    status: str
20	    detail: str
21	
22	
23	def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
24	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python3 -m artagents setup", description="Plan or apply ArtAgents local setup.")
25	    parser.add_argument("--apply", action="store_true", help="Apply local setup mutations. Default is dry-run.")
26	    parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Emit machine-readable setup output.")
27	    return parser
28	
29	
30	def plan_setup(*, apply: bool = False, project_root: str | Path | None = None) -> tuple[SetupStep, ...]:
31	    root = Path(project_root or REPO_ROOT)
32	    steps: list[SetupStep] = []
33	    steps.append(
34	        SetupStep(
35	            name="mode",
36	            status="apply" if apply else "dry-run",
37	            detail="local mutations enabled" if apply else "no files or dependencies will be changed",
38	        )
39	    )
40	    steps.append(_plan_agents_symlink(root, apply=apply))
41	    for action in _sync_managed_defaults(dry_run=not apply, overwrite=False, project_root=root):
42	        steps.append(SetupStep(name="elements sync", status="applied" if apply else "planned", detail=action))
43	
44	    registry = load_element_registry(project_root=root)
45	    for element in registry.list():
46	        result = install_element(element, project_root=root, dry_run=not apply)
47	        plan = result.plan
48	        if plan.noop_reason:
49	            steps.append(SetupStep(name="elements install", status="skipped", detail=f"{element.kind}/{element.id}: {plan.noop_reason}"))
50	            continue
51	        status = "applied" if apply else "planned"
52	        details = "; ".join(plan.command_lines())
53	        steps.append(SetupStep(name="elements install", status=status, detail=f"{element.kind}/{element.id}: {details}"))
54	    return tuple(steps)
55	
56	
57	def _plan_agents_symlink(root: Path, *, apply: bool) -> SetupStep:
58	    """Ensure AGENTS.md is a symlink to SKILL.md so both loaders read the same source."""
59	    agents = root / "AGENTS.md"
60	    target = "SKILL.md"
61	    skill = root / target
62	    if not skill.is_file():
63	        return SetupStep(name="agents.md symlink", status="warn", detail=f"{skill} missing; cannot link AGENTS.md")
64	    if agents.is_symlink() and (root / agents.readlink()).resolve() == skill.resolve():
65	        return SetupStep(name="agents.md symlink", status="ok", detail=f"AGENTS.md → {target}")
66	    if not agents.exists() and not agents.is_symlink():
67	        if apply:
68	            agents.symlink_to(target)
69	            return SetupStep(name="agents.md symlink", status="applied", detail=f"created AGENTS.md → {target}")
70	        return SetupStep(name="agents.md symlink", status="planned", detail=f"will create AGENTS.md → {target}")
71	    kind = "wrong symlink" if agents.is_symlink() else "regular file"
72	    if apply:
73	        agents.unlink()
74	        agents.symlink_to(target)
75	        return SetupStep(name="agents.md symlink", status="applied", detail=f"replaced AGENTS.md ({kind}) with symlink → {target}")
76	    return SetupStep(name="agents.md symlink", status="planned", detail=f"will replace AGENTS.md ({kind}) with symlink → {target}")
77	
78	
79	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
80	    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
81	    steps = plan_setup(apply=bool(args.apply))
82	    if args.json:
83	        payload = {
84	            "applied": bool(args.apply),
85	            "steps": [asdict(step) for step in steps],
86	        }
87	        print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
88	        return 0
89	
90	    print("ArtAgents setup")
91	    if not args.apply:
92	        print("dry-run: pass --apply to sync managed defaults and run local element install commands")
93	    for step in steps:
94	        print(f"[{step.status}] {step.name}: {step.detail}")
95	    return 0
96	
97	
98	if __name__ == "__main__":
99	    raise SystemExit(main())
100

1	"""Repository structure guardrails for ArtAgents canonical concepts."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	from dataclasses import dataclass
6	from pathlib import Path
7	
8	from artagents._paths import REPO_ROOT
9	from artagents.executors.folder import load_folder_executors
10	from artagents.orchestrators.folder import load_folder_orchestrators
11	
12	
13	LEGACY_PUBLIC_DIRS = ("conductors", "performers", "instruments", "primitives")
14	LEGACY_LOCAL_DIRS = ("performers", "conductors", "nodes", "instruments", "primitives")
15	INTERNAL_EXECUTOR_DIRS = {"__pycache__", "actions", "builtin", "bundled", "curated"}
16	INTERNAL_ORCHESTRATOR_DIRS = {"__pycache__", "bundled", "curated"}
17	TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_FILES = {
18	    "__init__.py",
19	    "__main__.py",
20	    "_paths.py",
21	    "doctor.py",
22	    "pipeline.py",
23	    "setup_cli.py",
24	    "structure.py",
25	    "theme_schema.py",
26	    "timeline.py",
27	}
28	TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS = {
29	    "__pycache__",
30	    "audit",
31	    "contracts",
32	    "domains",
33	    "elements",
34	    "executors",
35	    "orchestrators",
36	    "utilities",
37	}
38	
39	
40	@dataclass(frozen=True)
41	class StructureReport:
42	    errors: tuple[str, ...]
43	
44	    @property
45	    def ok(self) -> bool:
46	        return not self.errors
47	
48	
49	def validate_repo_structure(root: str | Path = REPO_ROOT) -> StructureReport:
50	    repo_root = Path(root)
51	    errors: list[str] = []
52	    errors.extend(_validate_legacy_dirs(repo_root))
53	    errors.extend(_validate_local_state_dirs(repo_root))
54	    errors.extend(_validate_top_level_artagents(repo_root / "artagents"))
55	    errors.extend(_validate_executor_folders(repo_root / "artagents" / "executors"))
56	    errors.extend(_validate_orchestrator_folders(repo_root / "artagents" / "orchestrators"))
57	    return StructureReport(errors=tuple(errors))
58	
59	
60	def _validate_legacy_dirs(repo_root: Path) -> list[str]:
61	    errors: list[str] = []
62	    for dirname in LEGACY_PUBLIC_DIRS:
63	        candidate = repo_root / "artagents" / dirname
64	        if candidate.exists():
65	            errors.append(f"legacy public package must not exist: {candidate.relative_to(repo_root)}")
66	    return errors
67	
68	
69	def _validate_local_state_dirs(repo_root: Path) -> list[str]:
70	    errors: list[str] = []
71	    local_root = repo_root / ".artagents"
72	    if not local_root.exists():
73	        return errors
74	    for dirname in LEGACY_LOCAL_DIRS:
75	        candidate = local_root / dirname
76	        if candidate.exists():
77	            errors.append(f"legacy local state directory must not exist: {candidate.relative_to(repo_root)}")
78	    return errors
79	
80	
81	def _validate_top_level_artagents(package_root: Path) -> list[str]:
82	    errors: list[str] = []
83	    for child in sorted(package_root.iterdir()):
84	        if child.name.startswith("."):
85	            continue
86	        if child.is_file() and child.suffix == ".py" and child.name not in TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_FILES:
87	            errors.append(f"top-level artagents module must move to a canonical package: {child.relative_to(package_root.parents[0])}")
88	        if child.is_dir() and child.name not in TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS:
89	            errors.append(f"top-level artagents directory is not a canonical concept: {child.relative_to(package_root.parents[0])}")
90	    return errors
91	
92	
93	def _validate_executor_folders(executors_root: Path) -> list[str]:
94	    if not executors_root.is_dir():
95	        return [f"missing executors directory: {executors_root}"]
96	
97	    errors: list[str] = []
98	    for folder in _public_child_dirs(executors_root, INTERNAL_EXECUTOR_DIRS):
99	        errors.extend(_require_files(folder, ("executor.yaml", "run.py", "STAGE.md"), root=executors_root.parents[1]))
100	        if _has_any(folder, ("orchestrator.yaml", "orchestrator.yml", "orchestrator.json", "orchestrator.py")):
101	            errors.append(f"executor folder contains orchestrator metadata: {folder.relative_to(executors_root.parents[1])}")
102	        try:
103	            definitions = load_folder_executors(folder)
104	        except Exception as exc:
105	            errors.append(f"invalid executor folder {folder.relative_to(executors_root.parents[1])}: {exc}")
106	            continue
107	        if not definitions:
108	            errors.append(f"executor folder emitted no executor metadata: {folder.relative_to(executors_root.parents[1])}")
109	            continue
110	        for definition in definitions:
111	            if definition.kind == "built_in" and definition.id.startswith("builtin."):
112	                expected = f"builtin.{folder.name}"
113	                if definition.id != expected:
114	                    errors.append(f"built-in executor {definition.id!r} must live in artagents/executors/{definition.id.removeprefix('builtin.')}")
115	            elif definition.kind == "external" and definition.id.startswith("external."):
116	                package = definition.metadata.get("package_id") or definition.id.removeprefix("external.").split(".", 1)[0]
117	                if package != folder.name:
118	                    errors.append(f"external executor {definition.id!r} package {package!r} must live in artagents/executors/{package}")
119	    return errors
120	
121	
122	def _validate_orchestrator_folders(orchestrators_root: Path) -> list[str]:
123	    if not orchestrators_root.is_dir():
124	        return [f"missing orchestrators directory: {orchestrators_root}"]
125	
126	    errors: list[str] = []
127	    for folder in _public_child_dirs(orchestrators_root, INTERNAL_ORCHESTRATOR_DIRS):
128	        errors.extend(_require_files(folder, ("orchestrator.yaml", "run.py", "STAGE.md"), root=orchestrators_root.parents[1]))
129	        if _has_any(folder, ("executor.yaml", "executor.yml", "executor.json", "executor.py")):
130	            errors.append(f"orchestrator folder contains executor metadata: {folder.relative_to(orchestrators_root.parents[1])}")
131	        try:
132	            definitions = load_folder_orchestrators(folder)
133	        except Exception as exc:
134	            errors.append(f"invalid orchestrator folder {folder.relative_to(orchestrators_root.parents[1])}: {exc}")
135	            continue
136	        if not definitions:
137	            errors.append(f"orchestrator folder emitted no orchestrator metadata: {folder.relative_to(orchestrators_root.parents[1])}")
138	            continue
139	        for definition in definitions:
140	            expected = f"builtin.{folder.name}"
141	            if definition.kind != "built_in" or definition.id != expected:
142	                errors.append(f"built-in orchestrator folder {folder.name!r} must expose id {expected!r}")
143	    return errors
144	
145	
146	def _public_child_dirs(root: Path, skipped: set[str]) -> tuple[Path, ...]:
147	    return tuple(sorted(path for path in root.iterdir() if path.is_dir() and path.name not in skipped and not path.name.startswith(".")))
148	
149	
150	def _require_files(folder: Path, filenames: tuple[str, ...], *, root: Path) -> list[str]:
151	    return [f"{folder.relative_to(root)} missing {filename}" for filename in filenames if not (folder / filename).is_file()]
152	
153	
154	def _has_any(folder: Path, filenames: tuple[str, ...]) -> bool:
155	    return any((folder / filename).exists() for filename in filenames)
156	
157	
158	__all__ = ["StructureReport", "validate_repo_structure"]
159

11	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
12	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Render an ArtAgents run audit report.")
13	    parser.add_argument("--run", type=Path, required=True, help="Run directory containing audit/ledger.jsonl.")
14	    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, help="HTML output path. Defaults to <run>/audit/report.html.")

16	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
17	    try:
18	        graph = build_graph(load_ledger(args.run))
19	    except FileNotFoundError as exc:
20	        raise SystemExit(str(exc))
21	    if args.json:
22	        print(json.dumps(graph, indent=2))
23	        return 0
24	    output = write_report(args.run, args.out)
25	    print(f"Wrote {output}")
26	    return 0

278	def _expand_external_command(
279	    executor: ExecutorDefinition,
280	    request: ExecutorRunRequest,
281	    values: Mapping[str, Any],
282	) -> tuple[tuple[str, ...], str | None, dict[str, str]]:
283	    if executor.command is None:
284	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"executor {executor.id!r} has no command")
285	    placeholders = _placeholder_values(executor, request, values)
286	    argv = tuple(_expand_placeholders(part, placeholders) for part in executor.command.argv)
287	    cwd = _expand_placeholders(executor.command.cwd, placeholders) if executor.command.cwd else None
288	    env = {[REDACTED](value, placeholders) for key, value in executor.command.env.items()}
289	    return argv, cwd, env
290	
291	
292	def _placeholder_values(executor: ExecutorDefinition, request: ExecutorRunRequest, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, str]:
293	    out = Path(request.out).expanduser().resolve()
294	    placeholders: dict[str, str] = {
295	        "out": str(out),
296	    }
297	    python_exec = _resolve_python_exec(executor, request, values)
298	    if python_exec is not None:
299	        placeholders["python_exec"] = python_exec
300	    brief = values.get("brief") or request.brief
301	    if brief is not None:
302	        brief_path = Path(str(brief)).expanduser().resolve()
303	        placeholders["brief"] = str(brief_path)
304	        brief_slug = str(values.get("brief_slug") or _default_brief_slug(brief_path, out))
305	        brief_out = out / "briefs" / brief_slug
306	        placeholders["brief_slug"] = brief_slug
307	        placeholders["brief_out"] = str(brief_out)
308	        placeholders["brief_copy"] = str(brief_out / "brief.txt")
309	    for key, value in values.items():
310	        if value is None:
311	            continue
312	        placeholders[key] = _stringify_value(value)
313	    for output in executor.outputs:
314	        output_path = _output_value(output, request, placeholders)
315	        placeholders[output.name] = output_path
316	        if output.placeholder:
317	            placeholders[output.placeholder] = output_path
318	    return placeholders
319	
320	
321	def _output_value(output: ExecutorOutput, request: ExecutorRunRequest, placeholders: Mapping[str, str]) -> str:
322	    if output.name in request.outputs:
323	        return _stringify_value(request.outputs[output.name])
324	    if output.placeholder and output.placeholder in request.outputs:
325	        return _stringify_value(request.outputs[output.placeholder])
326	    if output.path_template:
327	        return _expand_placeholders(output.path_template, placeholders)
328	    return str((Path(request.out).expanduser().resolve() / output.name).resolve())
329	
330	
331	def _resolve_python_exec(executor: ExecutorDefinition, request: ExecutorRunRequest, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str | None:
332	    input_override = values.get("python_exec")
333	    if _has_value(input_override):
334	        return str(input_override)
335	    if _has_value(request.python_exec):
336	        return str(request.python_exec)
337	    if not _executor_uses_placeholder(executor, "python_exec"):
338	        return None
339	    if executor.kind == "external" and executor.isolation.mode == "subprocess":
340	        installed_python = executor_python_path(executor)
341	        if installed_python.is_file():
342	            return str(installed_python)
343	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(
344	            f"executor {executor.id!r} requires an installed Python environment; "
345	            f"run `python3 -m artagents executors install {executor.id}` or pass python_exec as an input override"
346	        )
347	    return sys.executable
348	
349	
350	def _executor_uses_placeholder(executor: ExecutorDefinition, placeholder: str) -> bool:
351	    if executor.command is None:
352	        return False
353	    needle = f"{{{placeholder}}}"
354	    if any(needle in part for part in executor.command.argv):
355	        return True
356	    if executor.command.cwd and needle in executor.command.cwd:
357	        return True
358	    return any(needle in value for value in executor.command.env.values())
359

def _default_brief_slug(brief: Path, out: Path) -> str:
    generic_brief_names = {"brief", "plan", "prompt"}
    return out.name if brief.stem.lower() in generic_brief_names else brief.stem


def _stringify_value(value: Any) -> str:
    if isinstance(value, Path):
        return str(value)
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return ",".join(str(item) for item in value)
    return str(value)


def _required_input(inputs: Mapping[str, Any], key: str) -> str:
    value = inputs.get(key)
    if value in (None, ""):
        raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"{key} is required")
    return str(value)


def _optional_input(inputs: Mapping[str, Any], key: str) -> Any:
```

910	def resolve_timeline_theme(timeline: "TimelineConfig", themes_root: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
911	    """Return the merged theme view: theme.json + timeline.theme_overrides.
912	
913	    `timeline['theme']` is a slug resolved against `<themes_root>/<slug>/theme.json`.
914	    Overrides are deep-merged onto the loaded theme; list-valued fields (references,
915	    assets) are replaced wholesale by the override.
916	    """
917	    slug = timeline.get("theme") if isinstance(timeline, dict) else None
918	    if not isinstance(slug, str) or not slug:
919	        raise ValueError("Timeline.theme must be a non-empty slug")
920	    theme_path = Path(themes_root) / slug / "theme.json"
921	    if not theme_path.is_file():
922	        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Theme {slug!r} not found at {theme_path}")
923	    base = json.loads(theme_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
924	    if not isinstance(base, dict):
925	        raise ValueError(f"Theme file {theme_path} must contain a JSON object")
926	    overrides = timeline.get("theme_overrides") if isinstance(timeline, dict) else None
927	    if isinstance(overrides, dict) and overrides:
928	        return _deep_merge_theme(base, overrides)
929	    return base
930

964	def _assign_missing_arrangement_uuids(arrangement: Any) -> bool:
965	    if not isinstance(arrangement, dict):
966	        return False
967	    clips = arrangement.get("clips")
968	    if not isinstance(clips, list):
969	        return False
970	    used = {
971	        clip.get("uuid")
972	        for clip in clips
973	        if isinstance(clip, dict) and isinstance(clip.get("uuid"), str)
974	    }
975	    assigned = False
976	    for clip in clips:
977	        if not isinstance(clip, dict) or "uuid" in clip:
978	            continue
979	        value = uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
980	        while value in used:
981	            value = uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
982	        used.add(value)
983	        clip["uuid"] = value
984	        assigned = True
985	        order = clip.get("order")
986	        print(f"timeline.load_arrangement: migrated clip order={order} uuid={value}", file=sys.stderr)
987	    return assigned
988	
989	def load_arrangement(
990	    path: Path,
991	    pool_ids: set[str] | None = None,
992	    *,
993	    assign_missing_uuids: bool = False,
994	) -> Arrangement:
995	    data = json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
996	    migrated = False
997	    if assign_missing_uuids:
998	        migrated = _assign_missing_arrangement_uuids(data)
999	    validate_arrangement(data, pool_ids)

1	# Creating Tools
2	
3	Use this guide when ArtAgents is missing a capability.
4	
5	## Operating Level
6	
7	Start with the highest-level command that fits the user request. For normal
8	video creation, run an orchestrator through `python3 -m artagents` instead of
9	chaining internal executors by hand.
10	
11	Do not chain pipeline internals by hand unless you are debugging one specific
12	stage. Source-analysis executors intentionally pass file artifacts such as
13	transcripts, scenes, quote candidates, pools, timelines, and assets. Those files
14	make runs resumable and auditable, but they are not the right interface for a
15	creative request like "make a video about AI". Use the hype orchestrator or add
16	a new orchestrator for that workflow.
17	
18	Current start points:
19	
20	```bash
21	# Source-backed edit
22	python3 -m artagents --video source.mp4 --brief brief.txt --out runs/example --render
23	
24	# Audio-backed edit
25	python3 -m artagents --audio voiceover.wav --brief brief.txt --out runs/audio --render
26	
27	# Pure-generative edit from an existing brief
28	python3 -m artagents --brief examples/briefs/cinematic.txt --out runs/generative --render --target-duration 15
29	```
30	
31	If the user gives a topic instead of a brief, create or use a brief-generation
32	executor, then coordinate it from an orchestrator. Do not fake source media just
33	to satisfy a source-video path.
34	
35	## Build Order
36	
37	Before adding anything, follow this order. Move to the next step only when the
38	previous one cannot satisfy the request.
39	
40	1. **Try to compose existing executors.** Run `python3 -m artagents executors
41	   list` and `inspect` the likely candidates. If a workflow can be built by
42	   wiring existing executors together, write *only* an orchestrator that calls
43	   them. Do not duplicate logic that already lives in an executor.
44	2. **Create the missing executors.** Each new executor must do exactly one
45	   concrete unit of work — independently runnable, inspectable, testable. Keep
46	   it narrow: one network call, one transformation, one artifact in / one
47	   artifact out. Workflow shape, retries-across-stages, and conditional
48	   branching belong in the orchestrator, not the executor.
49	3. **Write the orchestrator that composes them.** It calls the executors
50	   (existing + new) and may call other orchestrators. Executors must not call
51	   orchestrators.
52	
53	Anti-pattern: a single orchestrator `run.py` that opens HTTP sockets, parses
54	model output, downloads files, and assembles grids — all inline. That is three
55	or four executors hiding in a trench coat. Split them out so each piece is
56	discoverable, reusable, and individually testable.
57	
58	## Decision Rule
59	
60	Create an **executor** when the missing capability performs one concrete unit of
61	work. It should be independently runnable, inspectable, and testable. Examples:
62	fetch Reigh data, render a timeline, upload a video, inspect audio, build a
63	sprite sheet, generate a brief from a topic, or transform one artifact into
64	another.
65	
66	Create an **orchestrator** when the missing capability coordinates a workflow.
67	It should call or plan child executors/orchestrators and keep business flow out
68	of individual tool implementations. Examples: hype pipeline, event-talk
69	workflow, thumbnail workflow, topic-to-video creation, or an understanding
70	dispatcher.
71	
72	Create an **element** when the missing capability is a reusable render building
73	block consumed by timeline JSON. Effects, animations, and transitions are
74	elements. If the user needs an editable visual primitive, fork or create an
75	element instead of hard-coding behavior in an executor.
76	
77	Create a **shared library** only when the code has no public runtime of its own.
78	Shared hype/editing concepts belong under `artagents/domains/hype`. Generic
79	plumbing belongs under `artagents/utilities`. Executor-specific helpers belong
80	inside that executor's optional `src/` package.
81	
82	For a one-off experiment, keep outputs and scratch files under `runs/`. Do not
83	create a public executor, orchestrator, or element unless the behavior should be
84	discoverable and reusable.
85	
86	## Common Friction Points
87	
88	**Too many required file paths.** This is expected for low-level executors.
89	Those paths are the artifact contract. Solve it by using an orchestrator, adding
90	a small helper executor for the missing artifact, or adding an orchestrator that
91	owns the whole flow. Only add literal/stdin conveniences when direct executor
92	use is itself the product surface.
93	
94	**Pool building rejects abstract or dialogue-light sources.** The source-video
95	hype path expects usable visual and dialogue candidates. If the goal is
96	abstract or purely generative, use the pure-generative path. If source-backed
97	abstract editing should be reusable, add an explicit orchestrator mode or a
98	focused executor change with tests rather than hand-editing triage and quote
99	JSON to force a pool.
100	
101	**No brief file exists.** Briefs are first-class input artifacts today. Use
102	`examples/briefs/` as samples. If the user repeatedly asks from a topic, add a
103	`builtin.generate_brief` executor and call it from a topic-to-video
104	orchestrator.
105	
106	**Render is missing assets.** Rendering consumes the timeline and assets pair
107	created by cut. Do not skip cut unless both `hype.timeline.json` and
108	`hype.assets.json` already exist for that brief.
109	
110	**No one-command topic creation.** The current one-command path starts from a
111	brief file. A topic-only command should be an orchestrator that provisions the
112	brief and then delegates to the existing hype or render flow.
113	
114	## Required Formats
115	
116	Executor folders use:
117	
118	```text
119	artagents/executors/<slug>/
120	  executor.yaml
121	  run.py
122	  STAGE.md
123	  src/              optional private helper package
124	```
125	
126	Orchestrator folders use:
127	
128	```text
129	artagents/orchestrators/<slug>/
130	  orchestrator.yaml
131	  run.py
132	  STAGE.md
133	  src/              optional private helper package
134	```
135	
136	Element folders use:
137	
138	```text
139	artagents/elements/bundled/<kind>/<id>/
140	  component.tsx
141	  schema.json
142	  defaults.json
143	  meta.json
144	```
145	
146	User-editable forks go under `.artagents/elements/overrides/<kind>/<id>/` and
147	should be created with:
148	
149	```bash
150	python3 -m artagents elements fork effects text-card
```
