164	def parse_ffprobe_fps(value: Any, *, path: Path | str) -> float:
165	    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
166	        return float(value)
167	    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
168	        raise SystemExit(f"ffprobe did not return fps for {path}")
169	    if "/" in value:
170	        numerator_text, denominator_text = value.split("/", 1)
171	        numerator = float(numerator_text)
172	        denominator = float(denominator_text)
173	        if denominator == 0:
174	            raise SystemExit(f"ffprobe returned invalid fps {value!r} for {path}")
175	        return numerator / denominator
176	    return float(value)
177	
178	def probe_asset(path: Path | str) -> dict[str, Any]:
179	    result = subprocess.run(
180	        [
181	            "ffprobe",
182	            "-v",
183	            "error",
184	            "-show_entries",
185	            "format=duration:stream=codec_name,width,height,avg_frame_rate,r_frame_rate",
186	            "-of",
187	            "json",
188	            str(path),
189	        ],
190	        check=True,
191	        capture_output=True,
192	        text=True,
193	    )
194	    try:
195	        payload = json.loads(result.stdout)
196	    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
197	        raise SystemExit(f"Invalid ffprobe JSON for {path}: {exc.msg}") from exc
198	
199	    streams = payload.get("streams")
200	    if not isinstance(streams, list):
201	        raise SystemExit(f"ffprobe did not return streams for {path}")
202	    stream = next((item for item in streams if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("width") and item.get("height")), None)
203	    kind = "video"
204	    if stream is None:
205	        stream = next((item for item in streams if isinstance(item, dict) and isinstance(item.get("codec_name"), str)), None)
206	        kind = "audio"
207	    if stream is None:
208	        raise SystemExit(f"ffprobe did not return a usable stream for {path}")
209	    format_info = payload.get("format")
210	    if not isinstance(format_info, dict):
211	        raise SystemExit(f"ffprobe did not return format metadata for {path}")
212	
213	    try:
214	        duration = float(format_info["duration"])
215	        width = int(stream.get("width") or 0)
216	        height = int(stream.get("height") or 0)
217	    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
218	        raise SystemExit(f"ffprobe returned incomplete metadata for {path}") from exc
219	
220	    codec = stream.get("codec_name")
221	    if not isinstance(codec, str) or not codec:
222	        raise SystemExit(f"ffprobe did not return a codec for {path}")
223	
224	    if kind == "video":
225	        fps_source = stream.get("avg_frame_rate")
226	        if fps_source in (None, "", "0/0"):
227	            fps_source = stream.get("r_frame_rate")
228	        fps = parse_ffprobe_fps(fps_source, path=path)
229	        resolution = f"{width}x{height}"
230	    else:
231	        fps = 0.0
232	        resolution = ""
233	    return {
234	        "type": kind,
235	        "duration": duration,
236	        "resolution": resolution,
237	        "fps": fps,
238	        "codec": codec,
239	    }
240	
241	def probe_video_duration(video_path: Path) -> float:
242	    result = subprocess.run(
243	        ["ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-show_entries", "format=duration", "-of", "default=noprint_wrappers=1:nokey=1", str(video_path)],
244	        check=True,
245	        capture_output=True,
246	        text=True,
247	    )
248	    return float(result.stdout.strip())
249	
250	def resolve_asset_paths(args: Any) -> tuple[dict[str, Path], dict[str, str]]:
251	    asset_paths: dict[str, Path] = {}
252	    asset_urls: dict[str, str] = {}
253	    raw_assets = getattr(args, "asset", None) or []
254	    for raw_entry in raw_assets:
255	        if not isinstance(raw_entry, str) or "=" not in raw_entry:
256	            raise SystemExit(f"Invalid --asset value {raw_entry!r}: expected KEY=PATH")
257	        key, raw_path = raw_entry.split("=", 1)
258	        if not key or not raw_path:
259	            raise SystemExit(f"Invalid --asset value {raw_entry!r}: expected KEY=PATH")
260	        if key in asset_paths or key in asset_urls:
261	            raise SystemExit(f"Duplicate asset key {key!r} in --asset")
262	        if asset_cache.is_url(raw_path):
263	            asset_urls[key] = raw_path
264	        else:
265	            asset_paths[key] = Path(raw_path).resolve()
266	
267	    video_path = getattr(args, "video", None)
268	    if video_path is not None:
269	        if "main" in asset_paths or "main" in asset_urls:
270	            raise SystemExit("Duplicate asset key 'main': provided by both --asset and --video")
271	        if asset_cache.is_url(video_path):
272	            asset_urls["main"] = video_path
273	        else:
274	            asset_paths["main"] = Path(video_path).resolve()
275	    audio_path = getattr(args, "audio", None)
276	    if audio_path is not None and video_path is None:
277	        if "rant" in asset_paths or "rant" in asset_urls:
278	            raise SystemExit("Duplicate asset key 'rant': provided by both --asset and --audio")
279	        if asset_cache.is_url(audio_path):
280	            asset_urls["rant"] = audio_path
281	        else:
282	            asset_paths["rant"] = Path(audio_path).resolve()
283	    return asset_paths, asset_urls
284	
285	def _url_cache_meta(url: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
286	    cache_path = asset_cache._path_for(url)
287	    if not cache_path.exists():
288	        return {}
289	    return asset_cache._read_meta(cache_path)
290	
291	def build_registry(
292	    asset_paths: dict[str, Path],
293	    asset_urls: dict[str, str],
294	    existing_registry: AssetRegistry,
295	    prior_meta: dict[str, Any] | None,
296	) -> tuple[AssetRegistry, dict[str, dict[str, Any]]]:
297	    registry: AssetRegistry = {"assets": {}}
298	    sources_meta: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
299	    existing_assets = existing_registry.get("assets", {})
300	    prior_sources = (prior_meta or {}).get("sources", {})
301	    for key, url in asset_urls.items():
302	        existing_entry = existing_assets.get(key) if isinstance(existing_assets, dict) else None
303	        cache_hit = isinstance(existing_entry, dict) and existing_entry.get("url") == url and (
304	            (
305	                existing_entry.get("type") == "audio"
306	                and existing_entry.get("duration") is not None
307	            )
308	            or (
309	                existing_entry.get("duration") is not None
310	                and existing_entry.get("resolution") not in (None, "")
311	                and existing_entry.get("fps") is not None
312	            )
313	        )
314	        prior_source = prior_sources.get(key, {}) if isinstance(prior_sources, dict) else {}
315	        if not isinstance(prior_source, dict):
316	            prior_source = {}
317	        carried = {field: prior_source[field] for field in CARRY_FORWARD_SOURCE_FIELDS if field in prior_source}
318	        sources_meta[key] = dict(carried)
319	        cache_meta = _url_cache_meta(url)
320	
321	        if cache_hit:
322	            if _FFPROBE_VERBOSE:
323	                print(f"ffprobe SKIP {key}")
324	            entry: dict[str, Any] = {
325	                "url": url,
326	                "duration": existing_entry["duration"],
327	                "type": existing_entry.get("type", "video"),
328	            }
329	            if entry["type"] != "audio":
330	                entry["resolution"] = existing_entry["resolution"]
331	                entry["fps"] = existing_entry["fps"]
332	            for field in ("content_sha256", "etag"):
333	                value = cache_meta.get(field, existing_entry.get(field))
334	                if isinstance(value, str) and value:
335	                    entry[field] = value
336	            registry["assets"][key] = entry
337	            continue
338	
339	        if _FFPROBE_VERBOSE:
340	            print(f"ffprobe RUN {key}")
341	        probed = probe_asset(url)
342	        probed_type = probed.get("type", "video")
343	        entry = {
344	            "url": url,
345	            "duration": probed["duration"],
346	            "type": probed_type,
347	        }
348	        if probed_type != "audio":
349	            entry["resolution"] = probed["resolution"]
350	            entry["fps"] = probed["fps"]
351	        for field in ("content_sha256", "etag"):
352	            value = cache_meta.get(field)
353	            if isinstance(value, str) and value:
354	                entry[field] = value
355	        registry["assets"][key] = entry
356	        sources_meta[key]["codec"] = probed["codec"]
357	
358	    for key, path in asset_paths.items():
359	        resolved_path = path.resolve()
360	        existing_entry = existing_assets.get(key) if isinstance(existing_assets, dict) else None
361	        cache_hit = (
362	            isinstance(existing_entry, dict)
363	            and existing_entry.get("file") == str(resolved_path)
364	            and existing_entry.get("duration") is not None
365	            and (
366	                existing_entry.get("type") == "audio"
367	                or (existing_entry.get("resolution") not in (None, "") and existing_entry.get("fps") is not None)
368	            )
369	        )
370	        prior_source = prior_sources.get(key, {}) if isinstance(prior_sources, dict) else {}
371	        if not isinstance(prior_source, dict):
372	            prior_source = {}
373	        carried = {field: prior_source[field] for field in CARRY_FORWARD_SOURCE_FIELDS if field in prior_source}
374	        sources_meta[key] = dict(carried)
375	
376	        if cache_hit:
377	            if _FFPROBE_VERBOSE:
378	                print(f"ffprobe SKIP {key}")
379	            registry["assets"][key] = {
380	                "file": str(resolved_path),
381	                "duration": existing_entry["duration"],
382	                "type": existing_entry.get("type", "video"),
383	            }
384	            if registry["assets"][key]["type"] != "audio":
385	                registry["assets"][key]["resolution"] = existing_entry["resolution"]
386	                registry["assets"][key]["fps"] = existing_entry["fps"]
387	            continue
388	
389	        if _FFPROBE_VERBOSE:
390	            print(f"ffprobe RUN {key}")
391	        probed = probe_asset(resolved_path)
392	        probed_type = probed.get("type", "video")
393	        registry["assets"][key] = {
394	            "file": str(resolved_path),
395	            "duration": probed["duration"],
396	            "type": probed_type,
397	        }
398	        if probed_type != "audio":
399	            registry["assets"][key]["resolution"] = probed["resolution"]
400	            registry["assets"][key]["fps"] = probed["fps"]
401	        sources_meta[key]["codec"] = probed["codec"]
402	    return registry, sources_meta
403	
404	def _tool_fingerprints(tools_dir: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
405	    # Short sha1 of each tool's source bytes — enough to detect "same code ran
406	    # this clip" without leaking filesystem paths or pretending to be semver.
407	    prints: dict[str, str] = {}
408	    for name in ("cut.py", "timeline.py", "transcribe.py", "scenes.py", "shots.py",
409	                 "triage.py", "scene_describe.py", "quote_scout.py", "pool_build.py", "arrange.py",
410	                 "text_match.py", "llm_clients.py", "quality_zones.py", "refine.py",
411	                 "inspect_cut.py", "enriched_arrangement.py",
412	                 "render_remotion.py", "open_in_reigh.py"):
413	        path = tools_dir / name
414	        if path.is_file():
415	            prints[name] = hashlib.sha1(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()[:10]
416	    return prints
417	
418	
419	def _ref_path_for_metadata(ref: Path | None, out_dir: Path) -> str | None:
420	    # Store refs relative to out_dir when the file lives inside it, so the
421	    # metadata moves with the run directory. Fall back to absolute otherwise.
422	    if ref is None:
423	        return None
424	    resolved_ref = ref.resolve()
425	    resolved_out = out_dir.resolve()
426	    try:
427	        return str(resolved_ref.relative_to(resolved_out))
428	    except ValueError:
429	        return str(resolved_ref)
430	
431	
432	def _quality_zones_ref_from_args(args: argparse.Namespace) -> Path | None:
433	    for ref in (getattr(args, "scenes", None), getattr(args, "transcript", None)):
434	        if ref is None:
435	            continue
436	        candidate = ref.resolve().parent / "quality_zones.json"
437	        if candidate.is_file():
438	            return candidate
439	    return None
440	
441	
442	def _sha256_for_path(path: Path) -> str:
443	    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
444	
445	
446	def _clip_bounds_for_duration(entry: dict[str, Any], duration: float, *, start: float | None = None) -> dict[str, float]:
447	    start_sec = float(entry["src_start"] if start is None else start)
448	    source_end = float(entry["src_end"])
449	    if start_sec < 0 or source_end < start_sec:
450	        raise ValueError(
451	            f"Invalid source bounds for pool entry {entry.get('id')!r}: "
452	            f"{start_sec:.3f}-{source_end:.3f}"
453	        )
454	    source_duration = max(0.0, source_end - start_sec)
455	    visible_duration = min(source_duration, duration)
456	    bounds = {"from_": start_sec, "to": start_sec + visible_duration}
457	    hold = max(0.0, duration - source_duration)
458	    if hold > 0:
459	        bounds["hold"] = hold
460	    return bounds
461	
462	
463	_TEXT_STYLE_FONT = "Inter, system-ui, sans-serif"
464	
465	
466	def _text_style_preset_to_attrs(preset: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
467	    if not isinstance(preset, str):
468	        return {}
469	    normalized = preset.lower().replace("_", "-")
470	    if "title" in normalized or "bold" in normalized:
471	        return {"fontFamily": _TEXT_STYLE_FONT, "fontSize": 64, "color": "#ffffff", "bold": True, "align": "center"}
472	    if "caption" in normalized:
473	        return {"fontFamily": _TEXT_STYLE_FONT, "fontSize": 36, "color": "#ffffff", "italic": "italic" in normalized, "align": "center"}
474	    if "closer" in normalized or "closing" in normalized:
475	        return {"fontFamily": _TEXT_STYLE_FONT, "fontSize": 48, "color": "#ffffff", "italic": True, "align": "center"}
476	    return {"fontFamily": _TEXT_STYLE_FONT, "fontSize": 36, "color": "#ffffff", "align": "center"}
477	
478	
479	def build_multitrack_timeline(
480	    arrangement: dict[str, Any],
481	    pool: dict[str, Any],
482	    registry: AssetRegistry,
483	    primary_asset: str | None,
484	    compiled_plan: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
485	    theme: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
486	    theme_dir: Path | None = None,
487	    theme_slug: str | None = None,
488	) -> TimelineConfig:
489	    clips: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
490	    if primary_asset is None and "rant" in registry["assets"]:
491	        clips.append(
492	            {
493	                "id": "clip_a_rant",
494	                "at": 0,
495	                "track": "a1",
496	                "clipType": "media",
497	                "asset": "rant",
498	                "from": 0,
499	                "to": float(registry["assets"]["rant"]["duration"]),
500	            }
501	        )
502	    for plan in compiled_plan or compile_arrangement_plan(arrangement, pool):
503	        order = plan["order"]
504	        at = plan["at"]
505	        duration = plan["duration"]
506	        audio_entry = plan["audio_entry"]
507	        overlay_entry = plan["overlay_entry"]
508	        if audio_entry is not None:
509	            clips.append(
510	                {
511	                    "id": f"clip_a_{order}",
512	                    "source_uuid": plan["uuid"],
513	                    "at": at,
514	                    "track": "a1",
515	                    "clipType": "media",
516	                    "asset": audio_entry["asset"],
517	                    **_clip_bounds_for_duration(audio_entry, duration, start=plan["audio_trim_start"]),
518	                }
519	            )
520	            # v1 shows the speaker — the audio source at the same timestamp.
521	            clips.append(
522	                {
523	                    "id": f"clip_v1_{order}",
524	                    "source_uuid": plan["uuid"],
525	                    "at": at,
526	                    "track": "v1",
527	                    "clipType": "media",
528	                    "asset": audio_entry["asset"],
529	                    "volume": 0.0,
530	                    **_clip_bounds_for_duration(audio_entry, duration, start=plan["audio_trim_start"]),
531	                }
532	            )
533	        if overlay_entry is not None:
534	            if overlay_entry.get("kind") == "generative":
535	                # extends prior plan Step 12
536	                # INERT until prior Steps 7+10+12 land — validate_pool currently rejects kind == 'generative'
537	                params = dict(overlay_entry.get("defaults", {}))
538	                if isinstance(plan.get("visual_params"), dict):
539	                    params.update(plan["visual_params"])
540	                effect_id = overlay_entry["effect_id"]
541	                # Brand-controlled effects own their entrance/exit choice via
542	                # themes/<theme>/effects/<id>/defaults.json. Drop any animation
543	                # arrays the LLM put in the brief — content lives in params, styling
544	                # lives in the effect.
545	                params = _drop_brand_animation_overrides(effect_id, params)
546	                # Fallback precedence: explicit plan duration -> theme pacing default -> legacy 4s default.
547	                generation_duration = float(plan.get("duration") or _theme_default_clip_sec(theme) or _LEGACY_DEFAULT_CLIP_SEC)
548	                # Pure-generative clips have no source media; omit source_uuid and
549	                # hoist orchestration metadata to the clip top level. The theme is
550	                # referenced once at top level (timeline.theme); leave per-clip
551	                # generation absent unless something actually diverges per clip.
552	                clips.append(
553	                    {
554	                        "id": f"clip_g_{order}",
555	                        "at": at,
556	                        "track": "v1" if plan.get("role") == "primary" else "v2",
557	                        "clipType": effect_id,
558	                        "hold": generation_duration,
559	                        "pool_id": overlay_entry.get("id"),
560	                        "clip_order": plan.get("order"),
561	                        "params": params,
562	                    }
563	                )
564	                continue
565	            overlay_play_duration = plan.get("overlay_play_duration") or duration
566	            clips.append(
567	                {
568	                    "id": f"clip_v2_{order}",
569	                    "source_uuid": plan["uuid"],
570	                    "at": at,
571	                    "track": "v2",
572	                    "clipType": "media",
573	                    "asset": overlay_entry["asset"],
574	                    "volume": 0.0,
575	                    **_clip_bounds_for_duration(overlay_entry, overlay_play_duration),
576	                }
577	            )
578	        text_overlay = plan["text_overlay"]
579	        if isinstance(text_overlay, dict) and isinstance(text_overlay.get("content"), str):
580	            text_data: dict[str, Any] = {"content": text_overlay["content"]}
581	            text_data.update(_text_style_preset_to_attrs(text_overlay.get("style_preset")))
582	            clips.append(
583	                {
584	                    "id": f"clip_t_{order}",
585	                    "source_uuid": plan["uuid"],
586	                    "at": at,
587	                    "track": "v2",
588	                    "clipType": "text",
589	                    "hold": duration,
590	                    "x": 0,
591	                    "y": 0,
592	                    "width": 640,
593	                    "height": 160,
594	                    "text": text_data,
595	                }
596	            )
597	    if not theme_slug:
598	        raise SystemExit(
599	            "build_multitrack_timeline requires a theme_slug — pass --theme so the timeline can reference it."
600	        )
601	    # If the source's resolution/fps doesn't match the theme canvas, surface that
602	    # via theme_overrides.visual.canvas so the renderer knows what to do. We only
603	    # write an override if it actually diverges from the theme.
604	    theme_overrides: dict[str, Any] = {}
605	    if (
606	        primary_asset is not None
607	        and primary_asset in registry["assets"]
608	        and theme is not None
609	    ):
610	        canvas = theme.get("visual", {}).get("canvas") or {}
611	        primary = registry["assets"][primary_asset]
612	        primary_resolution = primary.get("resolution")
613	        primary_fps = primary.get("fps")
614	        theme_resolution = (
615	            f"{int(canvas['width'])}x{int(canvas['height'])}"
616	            if isinstance(canvas.get("width"), (int, float)) and isinstance(canvas.get("height"), (int, float))
617	            else None
618	        )
619	        theme_fps = canvas.get("fps") if isinstance(canvas.get("fps"), (int, float)) else None
620	        canvas_override: dict[str, Any] = {}
621	        if primary_resolution and primary_resolution != theme_resolution:
622	            try:
623	                width_str, height_str = primary_resolution.split("x", 1)
624	                canvas_override["width"] = int(width_str)
625	                canvas_override["height"] = int(height_str)
626	            except ValueError:
627	                pass
628	        if primary_fps is not None and (theme_fps is None or float(primary_fps) != float(theme_fps)):
629	            canvas_override["fps"] = primary_fps
630	        if canvas_override:
631	            theme_overrides["visual"] = {"canvas": canvas_override}
632	    track_ids = {str(clip.get("track")) for clip in clips if clip.get("track")}
633	    if "a1" in track_ids:
634	        tracks = [
635	            {"id": "v1", "kind": "visual", "label": "Speaker"},
636	            {"id": "v2", "kind": "visual", "label": "B-roll"},
637	            {"id": "a1", "kind": "audio", "label": "Dialogue"},
638	        ]
639	    else:
640	        tracks = [{"id": "v1", "kind": "visual", "label": "Speaker"}] if "v1" in track_ids or not clips else []
641	        if "v2" in track_ids:
642	            tracks.append({"id": "v2", "kind": "visual", "label": "B-roll"})
643	        if "a1" in track_ids:
644	            tracks.append({"id": "a1", "kind": "audio", "label": "Dialogue"})
645	    config: dict[str, Any] = {
646	        "theme": theme_slug,
647	        "tracks": tracks,
648	        "clips": clips,
649	    }
650	    if theme_overrides:
651	        config["theme_overrides"] = theme_overrides
652	    # SD-009: every emitted timeline carries the canonical `output` block so
653	    # downstream consumers (Reigh's renderer, the publish CLI, etc.) get
654	    # resolution/fps/file without re-resolving the theme. Sourced from
655	    # theme.visual.canvas via materialize_output() in the shared schema package;
656	    # background/background_scale pass through from any prior timeline.output.
657	    if theme is not None:
658	        merged_theme = _merged_theme_for_output(theme, theme_overrides)
659	        config["output"] = materialize_output(config, merged_theme)
660	    return config
661	
662	
663	def _merged_theme_for_output(theme: dict[str, Any], theme_overrides: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
664	    """Apply theme_overrides.visual onto the loaded theme so materialize_output()
665	    sees the canvas the renderer will actually use (e.g. when the source asset's
666	    resolution/fps diverges from the brand canvas).
667	    """
668	    if not theme_overrides:
669	        return theme
670	    visual_override = theme_overrides.get("visual")
671	    if not isinstance(visual_override, dict):
672	        return theme
673	    merged = dict(theme)
674	    base_visual = dict(theme.get("visual") or {})
675	    canvas = dict(base_visual.get("canvas") or {})
676	    canvas_override = visual_override.get("canvas")
677	    if isinstance(canvas_override, dict):
678	        canvas.update(canvas_override)
679	    base_visual["canvas"] = canvas
680	    merged["visual"] = base_visual
681	    return merged
682	
683	
684	def _joined_segment_text(segment_ids: list[int], transcript: list[dict[str, Any]] | None) -> str | None:
685	    if transcript is None:
686	        return None
687	    parts = [
688	        str(transcript[index].get("text", "")).strip()
689	        for index in segment_ids
690	        if 0 <= index < len(transcript)
691	    ]
692	    joined = " ".join(part for part in parts if part).strip()
693	    return joined or None
694	
695	
696	def _pool_entry_caption_kind(entry: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
697	    return "dialogue" if entry.get("category") == "dialogue" else "visual"
698	
699	
700	def build_metadata_from_arrangement(
701	    arrangement: dict[str, Any],
702	    pool: dict[str, Any],
703	    registry: AssetRegistry,
704	    sources_meta: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
705	    args: argparse.Namespace,
706	    primary_asset: str,
707	    transcript: list[dict[str, Any]] | None,
708	    *,
709	    quality_zones_ref: Path | None = None,
710	    pool_sha256: str,
711	    arrangement_sha256: str,
712	    brief_sha256: str,
713	    compiled_plan: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
714	) -> PipelineMetadata:
715	    _ = registry
716	    clips: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
717	    for plan in compiled_plan or compile_arrangement_plan(arrangement, pool):
718	        order = plan["order"]
719	        audio_entry = plan["audio_entry"]
720	        overlay_entry = plan["overlay_entry"]
721	        rationale = plan["rationale"]
722	        if audio_entry is not None:
723	            audio_meta: dict[str, Any] = {
724	                "source_uuid": plan["uuid"],
725	                "pool_id": audio_entry["id"],
726	                "pool_kind": audio_entry["category"],
727	                "source_ids": dict(audio_entry.get("source_ids", {})),
728	                "arrangement_notes": rationale,
729	                "caption_kind": _pool_entry_caption_kind(audio_entry),
730	                "source_transcript_text": None,
731	            }
732	            if audio_entry.get("category") == "dialogue":
733	                segment_ids = audio_entry.get("source_ids", {}).get("segment_ids", [])
734	                if isinstance(segment_ids, list):
735	                    audio_meta["source_transcript_text"] = _joined_segment_text(segment_ids, transcript)
736	            clips[f"clip_a_{order}"] = audio_meta
737	            clips[f"clip_v1_{order}"] = {
738	                "source_uuid": plan["uuid"],
739	                "pool_id": audio_entry["id"],
740	                "pool_kind": audio_entry["category"],
741	                "source_ids": dict(audio_entry.get("source_ids", {})),
742	                "arrangement_notes": rationale,
743	                "caption_kind": "visual",
744	                "source_transcript_text": None,
745	            }
746	
747	        if overlay_entry is not None:
748	            if overlay_entry.get("kind") == "generative":
749	                clips[f"clip_g_{order}"] = {
750	                    "source_uuid": plan["uuid"],
751	                    "pool_id": overlay_entry["id"],
752	                    "pool_kind": "visual",
753	                    "arrangement_notes": rationale,
754	                    "caption_kind": "visual",
755	                    "source_transcript_text": None,
756	                }
757	                continue
758	            clips[f"clip_v2_{order}"] = {
759	                "source_uuid": plan["uuid"],
760	                "pool_id": overlay_entry["id"],
761	                "pool_kind": overlay_entry["category"],
762	                "source_ids": dict(overlay_entry.get("source_ids", {})),
763	                "arrangement_notes": rationale,
764	                "caption_kind": "visual",
765	                "source_transcript_text": None,
766	            }
767	        text_overlay = plan["text_overlay"]
768	        if isinstance(text_overlay, dict) and isinstance(text_overlay.get("content"), str):
769	            clips[f"clip_t_{order}"] = {
770	                "source_uuid": plan["uuid"],
771	                "pool_id": None,
772	                "pool_kind": "text",
773	                "arrangement_notes": rationale,
774	                "caption_kind": "visual",
775	                "source_transcript_text": None,
776	                "text_overlay_content": text_overlay["content"],
777	            }
778	
779	    out_dir = args.out.resolve()
780	    sources = {key: dict(value) for key, value in sources_meta.items()}
781	    if primary_asset is not None:
782	        primary_source = dict(sources.get(primary_asset, {}))
783	        primary_entry = registry["assets"].get(primary_asset, {})
784	        if isinstance(primary_entry.get("url"), str):
785	            primary_source["url"] = primary_entry["url"]
786	        primary_source["scenes_ref"] = _ref_path_for_metadata(args.scenes, out_dir)
787	        if args.transcript is not None:
788	            primary_source["transcript_ref"] = _ref_path_for_metadata(args.transcript, out_dir)
789	        if quality_zones_ref is not None:
790	            primary_source["quality_zones_ref"] = _ref_path_for_metadata(quality_zones_ref, out_dir)
791	        if args.shots is not None:
792	            primary_source["shots_ref"] = _ref_path_for_metadata(args.shots, out_dir)
793	        sources[primary_asset] = primary_source
794	    steps_run = ["cut"]
795	    if args.transcript is not None:
796	        steps_run.insert(0, "transcribe")
797	    if args.scenes is not None:
798	        steps_run.insert(-1, "scenes")
799	    if quality_zones_ref is not None:
800	        steps_run.insert(-1, "quality_zones")
801	    if args.shots is not None:
802	        steps_run.insert(-1, "shots")
803	    if args.arrangement is not None:
804	        steps_run.insert(-1, "arrange")
805	    return {
806	        "version": METADATA_VERSION,
807	        "generated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds").replace("+00:00", "Z"),
808	        "pipeline": {
809	            "steps_run": steps_run,
810	            "tool_versions": _tool_fingerprints(PACKAGE_ROOT),
811	            "config_snapshot": {
812	                "primary_asset": primary_asset,
813	                "renderer": args.renderer,
814	                "mode": "arrangement",
815	            },
816	            "pool_provenance": {
817	                "pool_sha256": pool_sha256,
818	                "arrangement_sha256": arrangement_sha256,
819	                "brief_sha256": brief_sha256,
820	                "source_slug": arrangement.get("source_slug"),
821	                "brief_slug": arrangement.get("brief_slug"),
822	            },
823	        },
824	        "clips": clips,
825	        "sources": sources,
826	    }
827	
828	
829	def arrangement_edl_rows(
830	    arrangement: dict[str, Any],
831	    pool: dict[str, Any],
832	    transcript: list[dict[str, Any]] | None,
833	    compiled_plan: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
834	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
835	    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
836	    for plan in compiled_plan or compile_arrangement_plan(arrangement, pool):
837	        source_entry = plan["audio_entry"] or plan["overlay_entry"]
838	        if source_entry is None or source_entry.get("kind") == "generative":
839	            continue
840	        source_ids = source_entry.get("source_ids", {})
841	        caption = None
842	        if source_entry.get("category") == "dialogue" and isinstance(source_ids, dict):
843	            segment_ids = source_ids.get("segment_ids")
844	            if isinstance(segment_ids, list):
845	                caption = _joined_segment_text(segment_ids, transcript)
846	        if caption is None:
847	            caption = (
848	                source_entry.get("text")
849	                or source_entry.get("subject")
850	                or source_entry.get("event_label")
851	                or ""
852	            )
853	        rows.append(
854	            {
855	                "asset": source_entry["asset"],
856	                "start": float(plan["audio_trim_start"]) if plan["audio_entry"] is not None else float(source_entry["src_start"]),
857	                "end": (
858	                    float(plan["audio_trim_start"]) + float(plan["duration"])
859	                    if plan["audio_entry"] is not None
860	                    else float(source_entry["src_start"]) + float(plan["duration"])
861	                ),
862	                "caption": caption,
863	            }
864	        )
865	    return rows
866	
867	def write_edl(
868	    selected: list[dict[str, Any]],
869	    out_dir: Path,
870	    asset_paths: dict[str, Path],
871	    asset_urls: dict[str, str],
872	) -> Path:
873	    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
874	    edl_path = out_dir / "hype.edl.csv"
875	    with edl_path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
876	        writer = csv.writer(handle)
877	        writer.writerow(["order", "src_start", "src_end", "src_path", "caption"])
878	        for order, item in enumerate(selected, start=1):
879	            asset_key = item["asset"]
880	            src_path = asset_urls.get(asset_key) or str(asset_paths[asset_key].resolve())
881	            writer.writerow(
882	                [
883	                    order,
884	                    f"{float(item['start']):.3f}",
885	                    f"{float(item['end']):.3f}",
886	                    src_path,
887	                    item["caption"],
888	                ]
889	            )
890	    return edl_path
891	
892	
893	def _register_cut_outputs(
894	    *,
895	    out_dir: Path,
896	    stage: str,
897	    parents: list[str] | None = None,
898	    rendered_path: Path | None = None,
899	    metadata: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
900	) -> None:
901	    audit = AuditContext.from_env()
902	    if audit is None:
903	        return
904	    parent_ids = list(parents or [])
905	    outputs = []
906	    for kind, filename, label in (
907	        ("edl", "hype.edl.csv", "Edit decision list"),
908	        ("timeline", "hype.timeline.json", "Timeline"),
909	        ("assets_registry", "hype.assets.json", "Asset registry"),
910	        ("metadata", "hype.metadata.json", "Pipeline metadata"),
911	    ):
912	        path = out_dir / filename
913	        if path.exists():
914	            outputs.append(audit.register_asset(kind=kind, path=path, label=label, parents=parent_ids, stage=stage, metadata=metadata))
915	    if rendered_path is not None and rendered_path.exists():
916	        outputs.append(audit.register_asset(kind="render", path=rendered_path, label="Rendered hype video", parents=outputs, stage=stage, metadata=metadata))
917	    audit.register_node(stage=stage, label="Build cut artifacts", parents=parent_ids, outputs=outputs, metadata=metadata or {})
918	
919	def ensure_resume_mode_args(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
920	    conflicts: list[tuple[str, Any]] = [
921	        ("--scenes", args.scenes),
922	        ("--video", args.video),
923	        ("--shots", args.shots),
924	        ("--transcript", args.transcript),
925	        ("--primary-asset", args.primary_asset),
926	        ("--asset", args.asset),
927	    ]
928	    for flag, value in conflicts:
929	        if value not in (None, []):
930	            raise SystemExit(f"--timeline cannot be combined with {flag}")
931	
932	def rebase_registry_paths(registry: AssetRegistry, assets_dir: Path) -> AssetRegistry:
933	    rebased_assets: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
934	    for key, entry in registry["assets"].items():
935	        rebased_entry = dict(entry)
936	        file_value = rebased_entry.get("file")
937	        if isinstance(file_value, str):
938	            resolved = Path(file_value)
939	            if not resolved.is_absolute():
940	                rebased_entry["file"] = str((assets_dir / file_value).resolve())
941	        rebased_assets[key] = rebased_entry
942	    return {"assets": rebased_assets}
943	
944	def build_resume_metadata(
945	    config: TimelineConfig,
946	    prior_meta: PipelineMetadata | None,
947	    *,
948	    render: bool,
949	    renderer: str,
950	) -> PipelineMetadata:
951	    clip_ids = [clip["id"] for clip in config["clips"]]
952	    prior_clips = prior_meta.get("clips", {}) if isinstance(prior_meta, dict) else {}
953	    clips: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
954	    if isinstance(prior_clips, dict):
955	        for clip_id in clip_ids:
956	            clip_meta = prior_clips.get(clip_id)
957	            if isinstance(clip_meta, dict):
958	                clips[clip_id] = dict(clip_meta)
959	    prior_sources = prior_meta.get("sources", {}) if isinstance(prior_meta, dict) else {}
960	    sources = {key: dict(value) for key, value in prior_sources.items()} if isinstance(prior_sources, dict) else {}
961	    return {
962	        "version": METADATA_VERSION,
963	        "generated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds").replace("+00:00", "Z"),
964	        "pipeline": {
965	            "steps_run": ["cut"],
966	            "tool_versions": {"cut.py": "sprint3"},
967	            "config_snapshot": {
968	                "mode": "timeline_resume",
969	                "render": render,
970	                "renderer": renderer,
971	            },
972	        },
973	        "clips": clips,
974	        "sources": sources,
975	    }
976	
977	def run_resume_mode(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
978	    ensure_resume_mode_args(args)
979	
980	    timeline_path = args.timeline.resolve()
981	    if not timeline_path.is_file():
982	        raise SystemExit(f"Timeline file not found: {timeline_path}")
983	    source_dir = timeline_path.parent
984	    assets_path_in = args.assets.resolve() if args.assets is not None else source_dir / "hype.assets.json"
985	    if not assets_path_in.is_file():
986	        raise SystemExit(f"Assets file not found: {assets_path_in}")
987	
988	    config = load_timeline(timeline_path)
989	    registry = load_registry(assets_path_in)
990	
991	    # SD-009: backfill the canonical `output` block if the resumed timeline
992	    # was authored before materialize_output() was wired in. Resolves the
993	    # timeline's theme slug + theme_overrides via the workspace themes root.
994	    if "output" not in config:
995	        themes_root = WORKSPACE_ROOT / "themes"
996	        try:
997	            from ...timeline import resolve_timeline_theme
998	            merged_theme = resolve_timeline_theme(config, themes_root)
999	            config["output"] = materialize_output(config, merged_theme)
1000	        except (FileNotFoundError, ValueError):
1001	            config["output"] = materialize_output(
1002	                config,
1003	                {"visual": {"canvas": {"width": 1920, "height": 1080, "fps": 30}}},
1004	            )
1005	
1006	    missing_assets = sorted(
1007	        {
1008	            asset
1009	            for clip in config["clips"]
1010	            for asset in [clip.get("asset")]
1011	            if asset is not None and asset not in registry["assets"]
1012	        }
1013	    )
1014	    if missing_assets:
1015	        quoted = ", ".join(repr(asset) for asset in missing_assets)
1016	        raise SystemExit(f"Timeline references assets missing from registry: {quoted}")
1017	
1018	    out_dir = args.out.resolve()
1019	    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
1020	
1021	    timeline_path_out = out_dir / "hype.timeline.json"
1022	    assets_path_out = out_dir / "hype.assets.json"
1023	    metadata_path_out = out_dir / "hype.metadata.json"
1024	    save_timeline(config, timeline_path_out)
1025	    if out_dir == assets_path_in.parent.resolve():
1026	        save_registry(registry, assets_path_out)
1027	    else:
1028	        save_registry(rebase_registry_paths(registry, assets_path_in.parent), assets_path_out)
1029	
1030	    prior_meta_path = source_dir / "hype.metadata.json"
1031	    prior_meta = load_metadata(prior_meta_path) if prior_meta_path.exists() else None
1032	    save_metadata(
1033	        build_resume_metadata(config, prior_meta, render=bool(args.render), renderer=args.renderer),
1034	        metadata_path_out,
1035	    )
1036	
1037	    summary = f"timeline={timeline_path_out} assets={assets_path_out} metadata={metadata_path_out}"
1038	    if args.render:
1039	        from ..render.run import render as render_remotion
1040	
1041	        hype_path = render_remotion(
1042	            timeline_path_out,
1043	            assets_path_out,
1044	            out_dir / "hype.mp4",
1045	            project_dir=REPO_ROOT / "remotion",
1046	        )
1047	        summary = f"{summary} hype={hype_path}"
1048	    _register_cut_outputs(
1049	        out_dir=out_dir,
1050	        stage="cut.resume",
1051	        metadata={"mode": "timeline_resume", "render": bool(args.render), "renderer": args.renderer},
1052	        rendered_path=out_dir / "hype.mp4" if args.render else None,
1053	    )
1054	    print(f"wrote {summary}")
1055	    return 0
1056	
1057	def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
1058	    global _FFPROBE_VERBOSE
1059	
1060	    parser = build_parser()
1061	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
1062	    if args.timeline is not None:
1063	        return run_resume_mode(args)
1064	    theme_path = _resolve_theme_path(args.theme)
1065	    theme = load_theme(theme_path) if theme_path is not None else None
1066	    theme_dir = theme_root(theme_path).resolve() if theme_path is not None else None
1067	    theme_slug = _theme_slug_from_path(theme_path)
1068	    pure_generative = args.video is None and args.audio is not None
1069	    no_audio = args.video is None and args.audio is None
1070	    if args.scenes is None and not (pure_generative or no_audio):
1071	        raise SystemExit("--scenes is required unless --timeline is provided")
1072	    if args.arrangement is None:
1073	        raise SystemExit("--arrangement is required unless --timeline is provided")
1074	    if args.pool is None:
1075	        raise SystemExit("--pool is required unless --timeline is provided")
1076	    if args.brief is None:
1077	        raise SystemExit("--brief is required unless --timeline is provided")
1078	    if args.transcript is None and not no_audio:
1079	        raise SystemExit("--transcript is required unless --timeline is provided")
1080	    _FFPROBE_VERBOSE = bool(args.verbose)
1081	
1082	    scenes_path = args.scenes.resolve() if args.scenes is not None else None
1083	    out_dir = args.out.resolve()
1084	    if scenes_path is not None and not scenes_path.is_file():
1085	        raise SystemExit(f"Scenes file not found: {scenes_path}")
1086	    if args.video is not None and not asset_cache.is_url(args.video) and not Path(args.video).resolve().is_file():
1087	        raise SystemExit(f"Video file not found: {Path(args.video).resolve()}")
1088	    if args.transcript is not None and not args.transcript.resolve().is_file():
1089	        raise SystemExit(f"Transcript file not found: {args.transcript.resolve()}")
1090	    if args.shots is not None and not args.shots.resolve().is_file():
1091	        raise SystemExit(f"Shots file not found: {args.shots.resolve()}")
1092	    if not args.arrangement.resolve().is_file():
1093	        raise SystemExit(f"Arrangement file not found: {args.arrangement.resolve()}")
1094	    if not args.pool.resolve().is_file():

27	def _pick_free_port() -> int:
28	    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as sock:
29	        sock.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))

32	
33	class _RangeHTTPRequestHandler(SimpleHTTPRequestHandler):
34	    """SimpleHTTPRequestHandler with HTTP Range support.
35	
36	    Remotion's media components seek into long source videos via Range
37	    requests. Without this, a 2-hour source video gets fully downloaded
38	    on every seek, which either times out or renders as black/silence.
39	    """
40	
41	    def log_message(self, format: str, *args) -> None:  # noqa: A002
42	        # Quiet the access log; one line per clip byte fetch is noise.
43	        return
44	
45	    def end_headers(self) -> None:
46	        self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*")
47	        self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET, HEAD, OPTIONS")
48	        self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Range, Content-Type")
49	        super().end_headers()
50	
51	    def send_head(self):
52	        path = self.translate_path(self.path)
53	        try:
54	            f = open(path, "rb")
55	        except OSError:
56	            self.send_error(404, "File not found")
57	            return None
58	        try:
59	            fs = os.fstat(f.fileno())
60	        except OSError:
61	            f.close()
62	            self.send_error(500, "File stat failed")
63	            return None
64	        size = fs.st_size
65	        range_header = self.headers.get("Range")
66	        if range_header and range_header.startswith("bytes="):
67	            try:
68	                start_s, end_s = range_header[6:].split("-", 1)
69	                start = int(start_s) if start_s else 0
70	                end = int(end_s) if end_s else size - 1
71	                if start < 0 or end >= size or start > end:
72	                    raise ValueError
73	            except ValueError:
74	                f.close()
75	                self.send_error(416, "Invalid Range")
76	                return None
77	            length = end - start + 1
78	            f.seek(start)
79	            self.send_response(206)
80	            self.send_header("Content-Type", self.guess_type(path))
81	            self.send_header("Accept-Ranges", "bytes")
82	            self.send_header("Content-Range", f"bytes {start}-{end}/{size}")
83	            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(length))
84	            self.end_headers()
85	            self._range_limit = length
86	            return f
87	        self._range_limit = None
88	        self.send_response(200)
89	        self.send_header("Content-Type", self.guess_type(path))
90	        self.send_header("Accept-Ranges", "bytes")
91	        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(size))
92	        self.end_headers()
93	        return f
94	
95	    def copyfile(self, source, outputfile) -> None:
96	        limit = getattr(self, "_range_limit", None)
97	        if limit is None:
98	            try:
99	                super().copyfile(source, outputfile)
100	            except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
101	                pass
102	            return
103	        remaining = limit
104	        chunk = 64 * 1024
105	        try:
106	            while remaining > 0:
107	                buf = source.read(min(chunk, remaining))
108	                if not buf:
109	                    break
110	                outputfile.write(buf)
111	                remaining -= len(buf)
112	        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
113	            pass
114	
115	
116	def _accepts_ranges(url: str) -> bool:
117	    request = urllib.request.Request(url, method="HEAD")
118	    try:
119	        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=20) as response:
120	            return response.headers.get("Accept-Ranges", "").lower() == "bytes"
121	    except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError):
122	        return False
123	
124	
125	def _parse_url_expiry(value: str) -> datetime:
126	    parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
127	    return parsed if parsed.tzinfo is not None else parsed.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
128	
129	
130	def _classify_assets(assets_path: Path) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
131	    if not assets_path.exists():
132	        raise FileNotFoundError("hype.assets.json missing — did you run cut.py first?")
133	    registry = timeline.load_registry(assets_path)
134	    classified: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
135	    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
136	    for key, entry in registry["assets"].items():
137	        url = entry.get("url")
138	        expires_at = entry.get("url_expires_at")
139	        if isinstance(expires_at, str) and _parse_url_expiry(expires_at) <= now:
140	            raise RuntimeError(f"Asset {key} URL expired at {expires_at}; refresh upstream before rendering")
141	        if isinstance(url, str):
142	            if _accepts_ranges(url):
143	                classified[key] = {"mode": "url-direct", "url": url, "local_path": None}
144	            else:
145	                classified[key] = {
146	                    "mode": "url-fetched",
147	                    "url": url,
148	                    "local_path": Path(asset_cache.fetch(url, expected_sha256=entry.get("content_sha256"))),
149	                }
150	            continue
151	        file_value = entry.get("file")
152	        if not isinstance(file_value, str) or not file_value:
153	            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Asset '{key}' has no file path or URL")
154	        local_path = Path(file_value)
155	        if not local_path.is_absolute():
156	            local_path = (assets_path.parent / local_path).resolve()
157	        classified[key] = {"mode": "local", "url": None, "local_path": local_path}
158	    return classified
159	
160	
161	def _server_root_for(assets_path: Path, classified: dict[str, dict[str, object]]) -> Path:
162	    """Pick a serve root that contains every asset file.
163	
164	    Uses the common parent of all absolute asset paths. Callers must ensure
165	    every asset resolves under this root before URL rewriting.
166	    """
167	    resolved_paths: list[Path] = []
168	    for entry in classified.values():
169	        if entry.get("mode") == "url-direct":
170	            continue
171	        local_path = entry.get("local_path")
172	        if isinstance(local_path, Path):
173	            resolved_paths.append(local_path.resolve())
174	    if not resolved_paths:
175	        return assets_path.parent
176	    common = Path(os.path.commonpath([str(p) for p in resolved_paths]))
177	    return common if common.is_dir() else common.parent
178	
179	
180	def _swap_from_dump(clip: dict) -> dict:
181	    out = dict(clip)
182	    if "from_" in out:
183	        out["from"] = out.pop("from_")
184	    return out
185	
186	
187	def _resolve_assets(
188	    assets_path: Path,
189	    server_root: Path,
190	    server_port: int,
191	    classified: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
192	) -> dict:
193	    # Remotion's bundler would copy the entire --public-dir into the webpack
194	    # bundle, which explodes disk usage for large source videos. We serve
195	    # assets over HTTP from their original location instead — Remotion's
196	    # <Video src> accepts http:// URLs natively and streams without copying.
197	    if not assets_path.exists():
198	        raise FileNotFoundError("hype.assets.json missing — did you run cut.py first?")
199	    registry = timeline.load_registry(assets_path)
200	    for asset_key, entry in registry["assets"].items():
201	        asset_info = classified[asset_key]
202	        if asset_info.get("mode") == "url-direct":
203	            entry["file"] = entry["url"]
204	            continue
205	        local_path = asset_info.get("local_path")
206	        if not isinstance(local_path, Path):
207	            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Asset '{asset_key}' has no local path")
208	        resolved = local_path.resolve()
209	        if not resolved.exists():
210	            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Asset '{asset_key}' resolved to missing file: {resolved}")
211	        try:
212	            rel = resolved.relative_to(server_root)
213	        except ValueError as err:
214	            raise RuntimeError(
215	                f"Asset '{asset_key}' at {resolved} is not inside server root {server_root}; "
216	                "all assets must share a common parent directory"
217	            ) from err
218	        entry["file"] = f"http://localhost:{server_port}/{rel.as_posix()}"
219	    return registry
220	
221	
222	def _validate_project_dir(project_dir: Path) -> None:
223	    if not project_dir.exists():
224	        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Remotion project directory not found: {project_dir}")
225	    package_json = project_dir / "package.json"
226	    if not package_json.exists():
227	        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Remotion project is missing package.json: {package_json}")
228	    node_modules = project_dir / "node_modules"
229	    if not node_modules.exists():
230	        raise FileNotFoundError("Run `npm install` in tools/remotion/ first")
231

233	def _serialize_timeline(timeline_path: Path, *, default_theme: str = "banodoco-default") -> dict:
234	    return timeline.Timeline.load(timeline_path).for_render(default_theme=default_theme).to_json_data()

237	def _resolve_theme_path(theme_path: Path) -> Path:
238	    if theme_path.name == "theme.json":
239	        return theme_path
240	    if theme_path.exists() and theme_path.is_dir():
241	        return theme_path / "theme.json"
242	    if theme_path.exists():
243	        return theme_path
244	    return WORKSPACE_ROOT / "themes" / str(theme_path) / "theme.json"
245	
246	
247	def _theme_for_props(theme_path: Path) -> dict:
248	    resolved = _resolve_theme_path(theme_path)
249	    if not resolved.exists():
250	        return {
251	            "id": "banodoco-default",
252	            "visual": {
253	                "canvas": {
254	                    "width": 1920,
255	                    "height": 1080,
256	                    "fps": 30,
257	                }
258	            },
259	        }
260	    theme = load_theme(resolved)

263	
264	def _theme_slug_for_render_default(theme_path: Path) -> str:
265	    resolved = _resolve_theme_path(theme_path)
266	    if resolved.name == "theme.json":
267	        return resolved.parent.name
268	    return resolved.stem or "banodoco-default"

270	
271	def _resolved_theme_for_render(timeline_path: Path, fallback_theme_path: Path) -> dict:
272	    """Resolve the timeline's theme + theme_overrides into the props-shaped dict.
273	
274	    The timeline references a theme by slug; per-run overrides live in
275	    timeline.theme_overrides. We merge them and trim to {id, visual} for Remotion
276	    props.
277	    """
278	    loaded = timeline.Timeline.load(timeline_path)
279	    render_view = loaded.for_render(default_theme=_theme_slug_for_render_default(fallback_theme_path))
280	    timeline_config = loaded.to_config()
281	    timeline_config.setdefault("theme", render_view.theme)
282	    themes_root = WORKSPACE_ROOT / "themes"
283	    try:
284	        merged = timeline.resolve_timeline_theme(timeline_config, themes_root)
285	    except (FileNotFoundError, ValueError):
286	        merged = None
287	    if not isinstance(merged, dict) or "visual" not in merged:
288	        # Caller-supplied --theme path is the fallback when the timeline can't be
289	        # resolved (e.g. running against a stripped fixture).
290	        return _theme_for_props(fallback_theme_path)
291	    return {"id": merged.get("id") or merged.get("visual", {}).get("id") or "theme", "visual": merged["visual"]}
292	
293	
294	def _regenerate_element_registries(project_dir: Path, theme_path: Path | None) -> None:
295	    generator = REPO_ROOT / "scripts" / "gen_effect_registry.py"
296	    cmd = [sys.executable, str(generator)]
297	    if theme_path is not None:
298	        cmd.extend(["--theme", str(_resolve_theme_path(theme_path))])
299	    subprocess.run(
300	        cmd,
301	        cwd=str(REPO_ROOT),
302	        capture_output=True,
303	        check=True,
304	        text=True,
305	    )
306	
307	
308	def _stderr_tail(stderr: str) -> str:
309	    lines = stderr.splitlines()
310	    tail = lines[-40:] if len(lines) > 40 else lines
311	    return "\n".join(tail).strip()
312	
313	
314	def _require_free_space(path: Path, min_free_gb: float | None) -> None:
315	    if min_free_gb is None or min_free_gb <= 0:
316	        return
317	    target = path if path.exists() else path.parent
318	    usage = shutil.disk_usage(target)
319	    min_free = int(min_free_gb * 1024 * 1024 * 1024)
320	    if usage.free < min_free:
321	        free_gb = usage.free / (1024 * 1024 * 1024)
322	        raise RuntimeError(
323	            f"Remotion render needs at least {min_free_gb:.1f} GiB free at {target}; "
324	            f"only {free_gb:.1f} GiB is available"
325	        )
326	
327	
328	def render(
329	    timeline_path: Path,
330	    assets_path: Path,
331	    out_path: Path,
332	    *,
333	    project_dir: Path | None = None,
334	    composition_id: str = "TimelineComposition",
335	    theme_path: Path | None = None,
336	    min_free_gb: float | None = None,
337	) -> Path:
338	    project_dir = project_dir or (REPO_ROOT / "remotion")
339	    _validate_project_dir(project_dir)
340	    _regenerate_element_registries(project_dir, theme_path)
341	    out_path = out_path.resolve()
342	    _require_free_space(out_path.parent, min_free_gb)
343	    props_path = (out_path.parent / ".remotion-props.json").resolve()
344	    classified = _classify_assets(assets_path)
345	    server_root = _server_root_for(assets_path, classified).resolve()
346	    try:
347	        port = _pick_free_port()
348	        handler = partial(_RangeHTTPRequestHandler, directory=str(server_root))
349	        server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", port), handler)
350	    except OSError as exc:
351	        raise RuntimeError(f"Permission denied (1100): local HTTP asset server blocked: {exc}") from exc
352	    server_thread = threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
353	    server_thread.start()
354	    try:
355	        resolved_registry = _resolve_assets(assets_path, server_root, port, classified)
356	        resolved_theme = theme_path or (WORKSPACE_ROOT / "themes" / "banodoco-default" / "theme.json")
357	        theme_for_props = _resolved_theme_for_render(timeline_path, resolved_theme)
358	        # The timeline references a theme by slug + optional theme_overrides;
359	        # theme.visual.canvas is the source of truth for Remotion calculateMetadata.
360	        merged_props = {
361	            "timeline": _serialize_timeline(
362	                timeline_path,
363	                default_theme=str(theme_for_props.get("id") or "banodoco-default"),
364	            ),
365	            "assets": resolved_registry,
366	            "theme": theme_for_props,
367	        }
368	        out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
369	        props_path.write_text(json.dumps(merged_props), encoding="utf-8")
370	        result = subprocess.run(
371	            [
372	                "npx",
373	                "remotion",
374	                "render",
375	                composition_id,
376	                "--props",
377	                str(props_path),
378	                "--output",
379	                str(out_path),
380	            ],
381	            cwd=str(project_dir),
382	            capture_output=True,
383	            check=False,
384	            text=True,
385	        )
386	        if result.returncode != 0:
387	            stderr_tail = _stderr_tail(result.stderr)
388	            message = f"Remotion render failed with exit code {result.returncode}"
389	            if stderr_tail:
390	                message = f"{message}\n{stderr_tail}"
391	            raise RuntimeError(message)
392	        props_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
393	    finally:
394	        server.shutdown()
395	        server.server_close()
396	    audit = AuditContext.from_env()
397	    if audit is not None:
398	        timeline_id = audit.register_asset(kind="timeline", path=timeline_path, label="Render timeline", stage="render_remotion")
399	        assets_id = audit.register_asset(kind="assets_registry", path=assets_path, label="Render asset registry", stage="render_remotion")
400	        render_id = audit.register_asset(
401	            kind="render",
402	            path=out_path,
403	            label="Rendered video",
404	            parents=[timeline_id, assets_id],
405	            stage="render_remotion",
406	            metadata={"composition": composition_id},
407	        )
408	        audit.register_node(
409	            stage="render_remotion",
410	            label="Render Remotion timeline",
411	            parents=[timeline_id, assets_id],
412	            outputs=[render_id],
413	            metadata={"composition": composition_id, "project_dir": str(project_dir)},
414	        )
415	    return out_path
416	
417	
418	def main() -> int:
419	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
420	    parser.add_argument("--timeline", type=Path, required=True)
421	    parser.add_argument("--assets", type=Path, required=True)

426	    parser.add_argument(

432	    try:

# Variants UX for ArtAgents Threads

## The core insight

The agent's working memory is the previous tool's stdout. So **the runner must emit, on every variant-producing run, exactly the line the agent needs to act on next.** Anything that requires the agent to *remember* is dead. Anything that requires it to *re-read* is fine, because we'll print it.

## Recommendation

### 1. Selection gesture: explicit, single command, one flag

`thread keep <run-id>:<n>` — that's it. No `--variant`, no `--artifact`, no path. The colon syntax is short enough the agent won't paraphrase it into oblivion, and the run-id is already in the `[thread]` prefix of the producing run, so the agent doesn't have to fish.

Reject the implicit "consume = pick" model. You named it correctly: it conflates picking from selecting, and breaks the "I like it but I'm not using it yet" case which is half of creative work. Picking is a claim about quality; consuming is a claim about pipeline. Don't fuse them.

Allow `thread keep <run-id>:2,4` for "both 2 and 4 are keepers" — multi-keep is real (two logo finalists to show a client).

### 2. Discovery: bake it into `[thread]` prefix, escalate when stale

Every run's prefix gets a second line when relevant:

```
[thread] logo-redesign · run #23 · parent #21:v3
[variants] run #21 produced 4 — none kept (use: thread keep 21:N)
```

The `[variants]` line appears on **every subsequent run** until the agent acts (keeps one, or explicitly dismisses with `thread keep 21:none`). Pollution is the right tradeoff: an unresolved variant group is technical debt and should nag.

### 3. The "ran it 4 times" failure: post-hoc merge, no auto-detection

Don't try to detect near-duplicate prompts — false positives will be infuriating and the heuristic will rot. Provide `thread group <run-id> <run-id> ...` for explicit retroactive grouping, and **mention it in the prefix when you see ≥3 sequential runs of the same executor with no `--variants` flag**:

```
[hint] 4 sequential generate_image runs — was this one attempt? (thread group 19 20 21 22)
```

Hint, not warning. Agent decides.

### 4. Default behavior on `-n 4`: structured line, never auto-pick

Your proposal is right. Emit:

```
[variants] run #23 produced 4 siblings (v1..v4) — none kept
```

Auto-grouping multi-output executors is obvious. Auto-picking is a category error. The structured line is the agent's full discovery surface.

### 5. Misalignment surfacing

- **Many variants, zero kept**: prefix gains `[thread-health] 12 variant groups, 0 kept — pick or dismiss`. Loud, persistent, easy to silence by acting.
- **Picked v3, passed v1**: do not auto-detect. Trust the agent; the contradiction will show in the iteration video and the agent (or human reviewer) will see it. Auto-correction here is paternalism.
- **Heterogeneous outputs (grid + candidates + manifest)**: this is the one place you need executor cooperation. Executors must declare their output role: `sibling`, `ancillary`, `index`. Only `sibling` outputs join the variant group. If an executor doesn't declare, **default everything to `ancillary` and emit zero variants** — fail closed. Force executor authors to opt in.

### 6. Iteration video minimum

One edge per attempt: `kept_variant → next_run.parent`. That's the entire data model the video needs. The `thread keep` command writes that edge. If the agent skips `keep`, the video shows a fan of 4 with no selection — which is *honest*, and visually communicates "we never decided." That's actually a feature.

### 7. Ergonomic minimum

**One command, optionally zero.** Per attempt, the agent runs at most:

```
thread keep <run-id>:<n>
```

Zero if the run had `-n 1` (no variants to pick). One if it had `-n >1`. Never two. The `[variants]` prefix line tells the agent exactly what to type — copy-paste from tool output to next tool call. That's the only interaction pattern that survives no-memory agents.

## Summary of emitted lines

- `[thread] <pursuit> · run #N · parent #M[:vK]` — always
- `[variants] run #N produced K siblings (v1..vK) — none kept` — when unresolved variants exist upstream
- `[hint] N sequential <executor> runs — was this one attempt? (thread group ...)` — soft nudge on suspected manual fan-out
- `[thread-health] X variant groups, 0 kept — pick or dismiss` — when rot accumulates

## Summary of agent commands

- `thread keep <run-id>:<n>[,<n>...]` — pick keeper(s)
- `thread keep <run-id>:none` — explicit dismiss
- `thread group <run-id> <run-id> ...` — retroactive sibling grouping
- `--variants N` on any executor — request a sibling group up front
- `--from <run-id>:<n>` on any executor — consume a specific kept variant (defaults to the most recent kept variant in the thread if omitted)

Five gestures total, one of them mandatory-per-attempt, the rest situational. That's the floor. Go below it and the variants layer becomes invisible to the agent; go above it and the agent skips it.

124	def _http_post_json(url: str, headers: dict[str, str], payload: dict[str, Any], *, timeout: int = 120) -> dict[str, Any]:
125	    body = json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8")

190	    except HTTPError as exc:

192	        raise SystemExit(f"Fireworks chat call failed ({exc.code}): {detail}") from exc
193	    except URLError as exc:

198	def parse_concepts(response: dict[str, Any], *, count: int) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
199	    choices = response.get("choices") or []
200	    if not choices:
201	        raise SystemExit("Fireworks response had no choices")
202	    content = (choices[0].get("message") or {}).get("content") or ""
203	    text = content.strip()
204	    try:
205	        data = json.loads(text)
206	    except json.JSONDecodeError:
207	        match = re.search(r"\{.*\}", text, flags=re.DOTALL)
208	        if not match:
209	            raise SystemExit(f"Could not parse JSON from Fireworks response: {text[:300]}")
210	        data = json.loads(match.group(0))
211	    raw_concepts = data.get("concepts") if isinstance(data, dict) else None
212	    if not isinstance(raw_concepts, list) or not raw_concepts:
213	        raise SystemExit(f"Fireworks response missing 'concepts' list: {text[:300]}")
214	    concepts: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
215	    for index, item in enumerate(raw_concepts[:count], start=1):
216	        if not isinstance(item, dict):
217	            continue
218	        prompt = str(item.get("prompt") or "").strip()
219	        if not prompt:
220	            continue
221	        concepts.append(
222	            {
223	                "candidate_id": f"logo-{index:03d}",
224	                "index": index,
225	                "name": str(item.get("name") or f"Concept {index}").strip(),
226	                "rationale": str(item.get("rationale") or "").strip(),
227	                "prompt": prompt,
228	            }
229	        )
230	    if not concepts:
231	        raise SystemExit("Fireworks response yielded no usable concepts")
232	    return concepts

268	def submit_fal_job(provider: str, payload: dict[str, Any], api_key: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
269	    model_id = PROVIDER_MODEL_IDS[provider]
270	    url = f"{FAL_QUEUE_URL}/{model_id}"
271	    headers = {"authorization": f"Key {api_key}"}
272	    try:
273	        return _http_post_json(url, headers, payload, timeout=120)
274	    except HTTPError as exc:
275	        detail = exc.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace") if exc.fp else ""
276	        raise SystemExit(f"fal submit failed ({exc.code}) for {model_id}: {detail}") from exc
277	    except URLError as exc:
278	        raise SystemExit(f"fal submit failed for {model_id}: {exc}") from exc
279	
280	
281	def poll_fal_result(submission: dict[str, Any], api_key: str, *, max_wait_sec: int = 300) -> dict[str, Any]:
282	    status_url = submission.get("status_url")
283	    response_url = submission.get("response_url")
284	    if not status_url or not response_url:
285	        raise SystemExit(f"fal submission missing status_url/response_url: {submission}")
286	    headers = {"authorization": f"Key {api_key}"}
287	    deadline = time.monotonic() + max_wait_sec
288	    delay = 2.0
289	    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
290	        try:
291	            status = _http_get_json(status_url, headers, timeout=30)
292	        except HTTPError as exc:
293	            detail = exc.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace") if exc.fp else ""
294	            raise SystemExit(f"fal status poll failed ({exc.code}): {detail}") from exc
295	        state = str(status.get("status") or "").upper()
296	        if state in {"COMPLETED", "OK"}:
297	            return _http_get_json(response_url, headers, timeout=60)
298	        if state in {"FAILED", "ERROR", "CANCELLED"}:
299	            raise SystemExit(f"fal job {state}: {status}")
300	        time.sleep(delay)
301	        delay = min(delay * 1.4, 8.0)
302	    raise SystemExit(f"fal job timed out after {max_wait_sec}s; last status_url={status_url}")
303

305	def _ext_for_format(output_format: str) -> str:
306	    return "jpg" if output_format in ("jpeg", "jpg") else output_format
307

309	def _save_first_image(result: dict[str, Any], dest: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
310	    images = result.get("images") or []
311	    if not images:
312	        raise SystemExit(f"fal result had no images: {result}")
313	    first = images[0]
314	    url = first.get("url")
315	    if not url:
316	        raise SystemExit(f"fal image entry missing url: {first}")
317	    data = _http_get_bytes(url, timeout=180)
318	    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
319	    dest.write_bytes(data)
320	    return {
321	        "path": str(dest),
322	        "source_url": url,
323	        "width": first.get("width"),
324	        "height": first.get("height"),
325	        "content_type": first.get("content_type"),
326	        "bytes": len(data),
327	    }

330	def _placeholder_image(dest: Path, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
331	    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
332	    try:
333	        from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
334	    except ImportError:
335	        dest.write_bytes(b"")
336	        return {"path": str(dest), "placeholder": True, "reason": "Pillow unavailable"}
337	    image = Image.new("RGB", (768, 768), (24, 24, 28))
338	    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(image)
339	    try:
340	        font = ImageFont.truetype("/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial Bold.ttf", 28)
341	    except OSError:
342	        font = ImageFont.load_default()
343	    draw.text((24, 24), "[dry-run]", fill=(220, 220, 220), font=font)
344	    draw.text((24, 72), label[:48], fill=(180, 180, 180), font=font)
345	    image.save(dest, quality=90)
346	    return {"path": str(dest), "placeholder": True, "reason": "dry-run"}

349	def render_concepts(
350	    *,
351	    concepts: list[dict[str, Any]],
352	    layout: dict[str, Path],
353	    provider: str,
354	    image_size: str | dict[str, int],
355	    output_format: str,
356	    fal_key: str | None,
357	    dry_run: bool,
358	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
359	    ext = _ext_for_format(output_format)
360	    results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
361	    for concept in concepts:
362	        candidate_id = concept["candidate_id"]
363	        dest = layout["images"] / f"{candidate_id}.{ext}"
364	        if dry_run or not fal_key:
365	            generated = _placeholder_image(dest, concept["name"])
366	        else:
367	            payload = _fal_payload(provider, concept["prompt"], image_size, output_format)
368	            submission = submit_fal_job(provider, payload, fal_key)
369	            result = poll_fal_result(submission, fal_key)
370	            generated = _save_first_image(result, dest)
371	            generated["request_id"] = submission.get("request_id")
372	        results.append({**concept, "generated": generated})
373	    return results

376	def write_grid(results: list[dict[str, Any]], out_path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
377	    paths: list[tuple[Path, str]] = []
378	    for item in results:
379	        gen = item.get("generated") or {}
380	        path = Path(str(gen.get("path") or ""))
381	        if path.is_file() and path.stat().st_size > 0:
382	            paths.append((path, str(item.get("name") or item.get("candidate_id"))))
383	    if not paths:
384	        return {"path": None, "image_count": 0, "reason": "no images available"}
385	    try:
386	        from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
387	    except ImportError:
388	        return {"path": None, "image_count": len(paths), "reason": "Pillow unavailable"}
389	    cols = max(1, math.ceil(math.sqrt(len(paths))))
390	    rows = math.ceil(len(paths) / cols)
391	    tile = 384
392	    label_h = 28
393	    grid = Image.new("RGB", (cols * tile, rows * (tile + label_h)), (16, 16, 18))
394	    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(grid)
395	    try:
396	        font = ImageFont.truetype("/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial.ttf", 16)
397	    except OSError:
398	        font = ImageFont.load_default()
399	    for index, (path, label) in enumerate(paths):
400	        col = index % cols
401	        row = index // cols
402	        x = col * tile
403	        y = row * (tile + label_h)
404	        try:
405	            with Image.open(path) as opened:
406	                tile_image = opened.convert("RGB")
407	                tile_image.thumbnail((tile, tile))
408	                offset_x = x + (tile - tile_image.width) // 2
409	                offset_y = y + (tile - tile_image.height) // 2
410	                grid.paste(tile_image, (offset_x, offset_y))
411	        except Exception:
412	            draw.rectangle((x, y, x + tile, y + tile), outline=(60, 60, 64), width=2)
413	        draw.text((x + 8, y + tile + 4), label[:40], fill=(220, 220, 220), font=font)
414	    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
415	    grid.save(out_path, quality=90)
416	    return {"path": str(out_path), "image_count": len(paths), "cols": cols, "rows": rows}
417	
418	
419	def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
420	    parser = build_parser()
421	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
422	    _validate_args(parser, args)
423	
424	    layout = build_layout(args.out)
425	    image_size_payload = args.image_size if isinstance(args.image_size, str) else dict(args.image_size)
426	
427	    plan = {
428	        "tool": "logo_ideas",
429	        "version": 1,
430	        "mode": "dry-run" if args.dry_run else "run",
431	        "ideas": args.ideas,
432	        "count": args.count,
433	        "provider": args.provider,
434	        "fal_model_id": PROVIDER_MODEL_IDS[args.provider],
435	        "fireworks_model": args.model,
436	        "image_size": image_size_payload,
437	        "output_format": args.output_format,
438	        "out": str(layout["root"]),
439	    }
440	    write_json(layout["root"] / "logo-plan.json", plan)
441	
442	    if args.dry_run:
443	        concepts = _planned_concepts(args.ideas, args.count)
444	        concepts_payload = {"mode": "dry-run", "raw_response": None, "concepts": concepts}
445	    else:
446	        fireworks_key = _load_env_var("FIREWORKS_API_KEY", args.env_file)
447	        response = call_fireworks_concepts(
448	            ideas=args.ideas,
449	            count=args.count,
450	            model=args.model,
451	            api_key=fireworks_key,
452	        )
453	        concepts = parse_concepts(response, count=args.count)
454	        concepts_payload = {
455	            "mode": "run",
456	            "model": args.model,
457	            "usage": response.get("usage"),
458	            "concepts": concepts,
459	        }
460	    write_json(layout["root"] / "concepts.json", concepts_payload)
461	    write_json(
462	        layout["root"] / "prompts.json",
463	        {
464	            "provider": args.provider,
465	            "fal_model_id": PROVIDER_MODEL_IDS[args.provider],
466	            "image_size": image_size_payload,
467	            "output_format": args.output_format,
468	            "prompts": [
469	                {"candidate_id": c["candidate_id"], "name": c["name"], "prompt": c["prompt"]}
470	                for c in concepts
471	            ],
472	        },
473	    )
474	
475	    fal_key = None if args.dry_run else _load_env_var("FAL_KEY", args.env_file)
476	    results = render_concepts(
477	        concepts=concepts,
478	        layout=layout,
479	        provider=args.provider,
480	        image_size=image_size_payload,
481	        output_format=args.output_format,
482	        fal_key=fal_key,
483	        dry_run=args.dry_run,
484	    )
485	
486	    grid = write_grid(results, layout["root"] / "grid.jpg")
487	    manifest = {
488	        "version": 1,
489	        "mode": plan["mode"],
490	        "ideas": args.ideas,
491	        "count": args.count,
492	        "provider": args.provider,
493	        "fireworks_model": args.model,
494	        "image_size": image_size_payload,
495	        "grid": grid,
496	        "candidates": results,
497	    }
498	    write_json(layout["root"] / "logo-manifest.json", manifest)
499	
500	    print(f"wrote_logo_manifest={layout['root'] / 'logo-manifest.json'}")
501	    if grid.get("path"):
502	        print(f"wrote_grid={grid['path']}")
503	    return 0
504	
505	
506	if __name__ == "__main__":
507	    raise SystemExit(main())
508

3	from __future__ import annotations

6	import subprocess

9	
10	
11	def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
12	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Run Moirae against a screenplay.")
13	    parser.add_argument("screenplay", type=Path)
14	    parser.add_argument("-o", "--output", required=True)

11	def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:

14	    parser.add_argument("workflow", type=Path)
